     6	/**
     7	 * Operation-scoped mutation lock (plan §Concurrency, Lock model).
     8	 * Acquire = atomic non-recursive mkdir of .handoff/lock/; exclusivity comes from
     9	 * EEXIST. Ownership facts live in owner.json; logical session ownership lives in
    10	 * bundle.json, never here. Takeover only when the owner is provably dead on this
    11	 * host — every other contended state refuses, and `force` never changes that.
    12	 */
    13	
    14	export class LockHeldError extends Error {
    15	  constructor(/** @type {string} */ message) {
    16	    super(message);
    17	    this.name = 'LockHeldError';
    18	  }
    19	}
    20	
    21	export class FencingError extends Error {
    22	  constructor(/** @type {string} */ message) {
    23	    super(message);
    24	    this.name = 'FencingError';
    25	  }
    26	}
    27	
    28	/** @param {string} root */
    29	function lockPaths(root) {
    30	  const dir = `${root}/.handoff`;
    31	  return {
    32	    dir,
    33	    lockDir: `${dir}/lock`,
    34	    owner: `${dir}/lock/owner.json`,
    35	    journal: `${dir}/journal.ndjson`,
    36	  };
    37	}
    38	
    39	const ESCAPE_HATCH =
    40	  'Verify manually that no baton process still holds it, then delete .handoff/lock to clear.';
    41	
    42	/**
    43	 * Inspect the current lock state.
    44	 * @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof lockPaths>} p
    45	 * @returns {{state: 'free'} | {state: 'torn', cause: string} | {state: 'live'|'dead'|'cross-host', owner: any}}
    46	 */
    47	function inspect(io, p) {
    48	  if (!io.fs.existsSync(p.lockDir)) return { state: 'free' };
    49	  if (!io.fs.existsSync(p.owner)) {
    50	    return { state: 'torn', cause: 'owner.json is missing (torn lock metadata)' };
    51	  }
    52	  let owner;
    53	  try {
    54	    owner = JSON.parse(io.fs.readFileSync(p.owner, 'utf8'));
    55	  } catch {
    56	    return { state: 'torn', cause: 'owner.json is unparseable (torn lock metadata)' };
    57	  }
    58	  if (owner.host !== io.host) return { state: 'cross-host', owner };
    59	  if (io.processAlive(owner.pid, owner.startTime)) return { state: 'live', owner };
    60	  return { state: 'dead', owner };
    61	}
    62	
    63	/** @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof lockPaths>} p @param {string} text */
    64	function journalNote(io, p, text) {
    65	  io.fs.mkdirSync(p.dir, { recursive: true });
    66	  // Allocate seq + dedupeKey like every other journal entry (gate-2 fix,
    67	  // reviewer-b finding 2): a keyless, seq-less note poisons journalSeq to NaN
    68	  // on replay and dedupes every later keyless event. We are already inside the
    69	  // lock here, so reading the tail for allocation is race-free.
    70	  let tailSeq = 0;
    71	  try {
    72	    const snap = JSON.parse(io.fs.readFileSync(`${p.dir}/bundle.json`, 'utf8'));
    73	    if (typeof snap?.journalSeq === 'number') tailSeq = snap.journalSeq;
    74	  } catch {
    75	    // no snapshot yet — journal tail below still counts
    76	  }
    77	  try {
    78	    for (const e of readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal).entries) {
    79	      if (typeof e.seq === 'number' && e.seq > tailSeq) tailSeq = e.seq;
    80	    }
    81	  } catch {
    82	    // no journal yet
    83	  }
    84	  const ts = io.now();
    85	  appendEntry(io.fs, p.journal, {
    86	    seq: tailSeq + 1,
    87	    ts,
    88	    type: 'note',
    89	    dedupeKey: dedupeKey({ ts, type: 'note', source: 'lock', text }),
    90	    writerId: ['lock', io.host, io.pid].join('-'),
    91	    source: 'lock',
    92	    payload: { text },
    93	  });
    94	}
    95	
    96	/** @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof lockPaths>} p */
    97	function publishOwner(io, p) {
    98	  const token = io.newFencingToken();
    99	  atomicWriteJson(io.fs, p.owner, {
   100	    host: io.host,
   101	    pid: io.pid,
   102	    startTime: io.startTime,
   103	    fencingToken: token,
   104	    acquiredAt: io.now(),
   105	    heartbeatAt: io.now(),
   106	  });
   107	  return token;
   108	}
   109	
   110	/** @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof lockPaths>} p */
   111	function acquire(io, p) {
   112	  io.fs.mkdirSync(p.dir, { recursive: true });
   113	  try {
   114	    io.fs.mkdirSync(p.lockDir);
   115	    return publishOwner(io, p);
   116	  } catch (err) {
   117	    if (/** @type {any} */ (err)?.code !== 'EEXIST') throw err;
   118	  }
   119	
   120	  const s = inspect(io, p);
   121	  switch (s.state) {
   122	    case 'free':
   123	      // Raced a release between mkdir failure and inspection; retry once. A
   124	      // competitor can win THIS mkdir too (gate-2 fix 12: observed under real
   125	      // barrier-released contention) — that is contention, not corruption.
   126	      try {
   127	        io.fs.mkdirSync(p.lockDir);
   128	      } catch (err) {
   129	        if (/** @type {any} */ (err)?.code === 'EEXIST') {
   130	          throw new LockHeldError('lock was re-acquired by a competitor mid-inspection — transient contention');
   131	        }
   132	        throw err;
   133	      }
   134	      return publishOwner(io, p);
   135	    case 'live':
   136	      throw new LockHeldError(
   137	        `lock is held by a live process (pid ${s.owner.pid} on ${s.owner.host}) — terminate that process first.`,
   138	      );
   139	    case 'cross-host':
   140	      throw new Error(
   141	        `lock is held from another host ('${s.owner.host}', this is '${io.host}') — cross-host locks are never provably safe to steal. ${ESCAPE_HATCH}`,
   142	      );
   143	    case 'torn':
   144	      // During ACQUIRE, torn metadata is usually a competitor caught between
   145	      // its mkdir and owner.json publication — transient by construction, so
   146	      // LockHeldError lets withLock wait it out (gate-2 fix 12). A genuinely
   147	      // crashed publisher exhausts the retry budget and surfaces this same
   148	      // message; recovery semantics (recoverLock) are unchanged: never steal.
   149	      throw new LockHeldError(`${s.cause} — unsupported for automatic recovery. ${ESCAPE_HATCH}`);
   150	    case 'dead': {
   151	      // Atomic dead-lock reclaim (gate-2 iter-2 B1, hardened iter-3 finding-1).
   152	      // rm-then-mkdir is NOT mutually exclusive: two contenders can both observe
   153	      // 'dead' before either removes the stale dir, then both rm+mkdir — the
   154	      // second silently clobbers the first, yielding two simultaneous holders and
   155	      // two takeover notes. rename() is the atomic arbiter instead: the stale
   156	      // lock dir has exactly one name, so only one process can move it aside (the
   157	      // rest get ENOENT). Winning the rename IS winning the reclaim; a contender
   158	      // that recreated the dir via the 'free' path first makes our mkdir EEXIST →
   159	      // transient contention. The fencing token stays the write-time backstop.
   160	      const aside = `${p.lockDir}.reclaim-${io.pid}-${io.newFencingToken()}`;
   161	      try {
   162	        io.fs.renameSync(p.lockDir, aside);
   163	      } catch (err) {
   164	        // Source already gone → another contender is reclaiming it; wait+retry.
   165	        if (/** @type {any} */ (err)?.code === 'ENOENT') {
   166	          throw new LockHeldError('a competitor is reclaiming the dead lock — transient contention');
   167	        }
   168	        throw err;
   169	      }
   170	      // Winning the atomic rename IS the takeover — exactly one process can, so
   171	      // journal it HERE, before recreating. If we then lose the fresh-mkdir race
   172	      // to a competitor that grabbed the freed slot via the 'free' path, the
   173	      // audit trail still records who cleared the stale lock (recording it after
   174	      // publish would drop the note in that interleaving — the clearer isn't
   175	      // always the eventual holder).
   176	      journalNote(io, p, `lock takeover: took over stale lock from dead pid ${s.owner.pid} (start time ${s.owner.startTime})`);
   177	      io.fs.rmSync(aside, { recursive: true, force: true });
   178	      try {
   179	        io.fs.mkdirSync(p.lockDir);
   180	      } catch (err) {
   181	        if (/** @type {any} */ (err)?.code === 'EEXIST') {
   182	          throw new LockHeldError('a competitor re-claimed the dead lock first — transient contention');
   183	        }
   184	        throw err;
   185	      }
   186	      return publishOwner(io, p);
   187	    }
   188	    /* c8 ignore next 2 */
   189	    default:
   190	      throw new Error('unreachable lock state');
   191	  }
   192	}
   193	
   194	/**
   195	 * Fence-guarded release: the lock dir is removed only while owner.json still
   196	 * carries OUR token. A holder that paused past a takeover must not delete the
   197	 * competitor's lock on its way out — release is a write like any other.
   198	 * @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof lockPaths>} p @param {string} token
   199	 */
   200	function release(io, p, token) {
   201	  try {
   202	    const current = JSON.parse(io.fs.readFileSync(p.owner, 'utf8'));
   203	    if (current.fencingToken !== token) return; // a competitor took over — their lock, not ours
   204	  } catch {
   205	    return; // owner metadata gone or torn mid-hold — never guess; leave it for recovery
   206	  }
   207	  io.fs.rmSync(p.lockDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
   208	}
   209	
   210	// A live same-host holder is transient by design — operation-scoped locks are
   211	// held for milliseconds — so contention waits instead of dropping the
   212	// operation (gate-2 fix 12: simultaneous checkpoints must all land). The budget
   213	// is generous (≈15 s): under heavy load a writer may be starved of scheduling
   214	// for seconds, and waiting is strictly safer than silently dropping its work.
   215	// Every other contended state (torn, cross-host) stays fail-fast: waiting
   216	// cannot make those provably safe.
   217	const DEFAULT_RETRY = { attempts: 600, delayMs: 25 };
   218	
   219	/** Synchronous sleep without burning a core: Atomics.wait on a throwaway buffer. */
   220	const sleepMs = (/** @type {number} */ ms) => {
   221	  Atomics.wait(new Int32Array(new SharedArrayBuffer(4)), 0, 0, ms);
   222	};
   223	
   224	/**
   225	 * Acquire → run fn(token) → release (also on throw). Synchronous by design:
   226	 * everything the lock guards is sync fs work. Live-held locks are retried on
   227	 * a bounded budget (override via io.lockRetry = {attempts, delayMs}).
   228	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io @param {(token: string) => any} fn
   229	 */
   230	export function withLock(root, io, fn) {
   231	  const p = lockPaths(root);
   232	  // Jail BEFORE any lock mutation (gate-2 iter-2 B2): a symlinked .handoff or
   233	  // .handoff/lock must never be created into or removed through — that would
   234	  // reach outside the repository. Checked once here, ahead of acquire.
   235	  const safe = checkHandoffTree(root, io);
   236	  if (!safe.ok) throw new Error(`${safe.problem} — refusing to acquire the lock`);
   237	  const retry = io.lockRetry ?? DEFAULT_RETRY;
   238	  /** @type {string} */
   239	  let token;
   240	  for (let attempt = 0; ; attempt++) {
   241	    try {
   242	      token = acquire(io, p);
   243	      break;
   244	    } catch (err) {
   245	      if (err instanceof LockHeldError && attempt < retry.attempts) {
   246	        sleepMs(retry.delayMs);
   247	        continue;
   248	      }
   249	      throw err;
   250	    }
   251	  }
   252	  try {
   253	    return fn(token);
   254	  } finally {
   255	    release(io, p, token);
   256	  }
   257	}
   258	
   259	/**
   260	 * Fast-abort layer: re-read the published token immediately before a write; a
   261	 * holder that paused past its last check aborts instead of clobbering.
   262	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io @param {string} token @param {() => any} fn
   263	 */
   264	export function guardedWrite(root, io, token, fn) {
   265	  const p = lockPaths(root);
   266	  let current = null;
   267	  try {
   268	    current = JSON.parse(io.fs.readFileSync(p.owner, 'utf8'));
   269	  } catch {
   270	    throw new FencingError('lock owner metadata is gone — fencing check failed; aborting write');
   271	  }
   272	  if (current.fencingToken !== token) {
   273	    throw new FencingError(
   274	      `fencing token changed (held '${token}', current '${current.fencingToken}') — another process took over; aborting write`,
   275	    );
   276	  }
   277	  return fn();
   278	}
   279	
   280	/**
   281	 * Explicit recovery. `force` is an intent flag only — it never changes the
   282	 * decision: exactly one state recovers (provably-dead same-host owner).
   283	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io @param {{force?: boolean}} [opts]
   284	 * @returns {{recovered: boolean, refusedReason: string | null}}
   285	 */
   286	export function recoverLock(root, io, opts = {}) {
   287	  void opts;
   288	  const p = lockPaths(root);
   289	  // Jail before recovery (gate-2 iter-2 B2): recoverLock removes the lock dir,
   290	  // so a symlinked managed tree must be refused first — even with force, a
   291	  // symlink is never provably safe to delete through.
   292	  const safe = checkHandoffTree(root, io);
   293	  if (!safe.ok) return { recovered: false, refusedReason: `${safe.problem} — refusing recovery` };
   294	  const s = inspect(io, p);
   295	  switch (s.state) {
   296	    case 'free':
   297	      return { recovered: true, refusedReason: null };
   298	    case 'dead':
   299	      // Provably-dead owner: removal is justified by the verified state itself,
   300	      // not by holding the fence (there is no live holder to fence against).
   301	      io.fs.rmSync(p.lockDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
   302	      journalNote(io, p, `lock recovery: cleared stale lock of dead pid ${s.owner.pid}`);
   303	      return { recovered: true, refusedReason: null };
   304	    case 'live':
   305	      return {
   306	        recovered: false,
   307	        refusedReason: `lock is held by a live process (pid ${s.owner.pid} on ${s.owner.host}) — terminate that process first.`,
   308	      };
   309	    case 'cross-host':
   310	      return {
   311	        recovered: false,
   312	        refusedReason: `lock is held from another host ('${s.owner.host}', this is '${io.host}') — cross-host locks are never provably safe to steal, even with force. ${ESCAPE_HATCH}`,
   313	      };
   314	    case 'torn':
   315	      return {
   316	        recovered: false,
   317	        refusedReason: `${s.cause} — unsupported for automatic recovery, even with force. ${ESCAPE_HATCH}`,
   318	      };
   319	    /* c8 ignore next 2 */
   320	    default:
   321	      return { recovered: false, refusedReason: 'unreachable lock state' };
   322	  }
   323	}
     1	// Threat-model guards (plan §Threat model): bundle-borne paths and the managed
     2	// .handoff tree are untrusted input. jailRelPath is the single pure gate every
     3	// repository path passes through before storage or render; checkHandoffTree
     4	// refuses to operate on a managed tree that contains symlinks or resolves
     5	// outside the repository root.
     6	
     7	const NUL = String.fromCharCode(0);
     8	
     9	/**
    10	 * Normalize an untrusted repository-relative path, or return null when it is
    11	 * unsafe: non-string, empty, NUL bytes, absolute (posix, Windows drive, UNC),
    12	 * or escaping the root via `..`. Backslashes are treated as separators so a
    13	 * Windows spelling cannot smuggle a traversal past a posix check.
    14	 * @param {any} p
    15	 * @returns {string | null}
    16	 */
    17	export function jailRelPath(p) {
    18	  if (typeof p !== 'string' || p.length === 0 || p.includes(NUL)) return null;
    19	  const unified = p.replace(/\\/g, '/');
    20	  if (unified.startsWith('/')) return null;
    21	  if (/^[A-Za-z]:/.test(unified)) return null;
    22	  /** @type {string[]} */
    23	  const out = [];
    24	  for (const seg of unified.split('/')) {
    25	    if (seg === '' || seg === '.') continue;
    26	    if (seg === '..') {
    27	      if (out.length === 0) return null;
    28	      out.pop();
    29	      continue;
    30	    }
    31	    out.push(seg);
    32	  }
    33	  if (out.length === 0) return null;
    34	  return out.join('/');
    35	}
    36	
    37	/** Thrown by store mutations when the managed tree fails the jail check. */
    38	export class UnsafeTreeError extends Error {}
    39	
    40	/**
    41	 * Verify the managed .handoff tree is safe to operate on: the directory itself
    42	 * is not a symlink, its realpath stays under the repository root, and no entry
    43	 * anywhere in the tree is a symlink (lstat walk — never follows links). An
    44	 * absent tree is safe; an unverifiable tree is refused, never trusted.
    45	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
    46	 * @returns {{ok: true} | {ok: false, problem: string}}
    47	 */
    48	export function checkHandoffTree(root, io) {
    49	  const dir = `${root}/.handoff`;
    50	  try {
    51	    if (!io.fs.existsSync(dir)) return { ok: true };
    52	    if (io.fs.lstatSync(dir).isSymbolicLink()) {
    53	      return { ok: false, problem: `.handoff is a symlink — refusing to operate through a linked managed tree` };
    54	    }
    55	    // Separator-normalize before comparing (Windows: realpathSync returns
    56	    // backslash paths, so a raw compare against `${rootReal}/.handoff` would
    57	    // mismatch on EVERY managed tree and refuse all operations). Comparing on a
    58	    // canonical forward-slash spelling makes containment platform-neutral.
    59	    const norm = (/** @type {string} */ s) => String(s).replace(/\\/g, '/');
    60	    const rootReal = norm(io.fs.realpathSync(root));
    61	    const dirReal = norm(io.fs.realpathSync(dir));
    62	    if (dirReal !== `${rootReal}/.handoff`) {
    63	      return { ok: false, problem: `.handoff resolves outside the repository root (${dirReal}) — refusing` };
    64	    }
    65	    // Walk is race-tolerant (gate-2 iter-2): under concurrent atomic writes
    66	    // the tree churns — tmp files, the lock dir, and rotated journals appear
    67	    // and vanish between readdir and lstat. An entry that disappears mid-walk
    68	    // (ENOENT) is normal concurrency, NOT a symlink attack, so it is skipped;
    69	    // only a POSITIVELY observed symlink refuses. The stable top-level checks
    70	    // above (.handoff itself a symlink / realpath escape) stay strict.
    71	    /** @type {string[]} */
    72	    const stack = [dir];
    73	    while (stack.length > 0) {
    74	      const d = /** @type {string} */ (stack.pop());
    75	      /** @type {string[]} */
    76	      let names;
    77	      try {
    78	        names = io.fs.readdirSync(d);
    79	      } catch (e) {
    80	        if (/** @type {any} */ (e)?.code === 'ENOENT') continue; // subdir removed mid-walk
    81	        throw e;
    82	      }
    83	      for (const name of names) {
    84	        const p = `${d}/${name}`;
    85	        let st;
    86	        try {
    87	          st = io.fs.lstatSync(p);
    88	        } catch (e) {
    89	          if (/** @type {any} */ (e)?.code === 'ENOENT') continue; // entry vanished mid-walk
    90	          throw e;
    91	        }
    92	        if (st.isSymbolicLink()) return { ok: false, problem: `${p} is a symlink — refusing (managed-tree jail)` };
    93	        if (st.isDirectory()) stack.push(p);
    94	      }
    95	    }
    96	    return { ok: true };
    97	  } catch (err) {
    98	    const msg = /** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? String(err);
    99	    return { ok: false, problem: `managed tree could not be verified (${msg}) — refusing` };
   100	  }
   101	}
   102	
   103	/**
   104	 * Assert form of checkHandoffTree for mutation paths: throws UnsafeTreeError
   105	 * so commands surface the refusal through their existing soft-failure handling.
   106	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
   107	 */
   108	export function assertHandoffTreeSafe(root, io) {
   109	  const r = checkHandoffTree(root, io);
   110	  if (!r.ok) throw new UnsafeTreeError(r.problem);
   111	}
     1	/**
     2	 * The --json contract: exactly one compact envelope line on stdout, nothing else.
     3	 * @param {any} io
     4	 * @param {{ok: boolean, data?: any, warnings?: any[], error?: {code: string, msg: string} | null}} env
     5	 */
     6	export function emitEnvelope(io, env) {
     7	  const full = { ok: env.ok, data: env.data ?? null, warnings: env.warnings ?? [], error: env.error ?? null };
     8	  io.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(full) + '\n');
     9	}
    10	
    11	/**
    12	 * Centralized usage-error emission (gate-2 fix 11): with --json the envelope
    13	 * contract holds even for bad invocations; otherwise a one-line stderr
    14	 * message. Always returns the usage exit code 2.
    15	 * @param {any} io @param {Record<string, string | boolean>} flags
    16	 * @param {string} cmd @param {string} msg
    17	 * @returns {number}
    18	 */
    19	export function usageError(io, flags, cmd, msg) {
    20	  if (flags.json === true) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'usage', msg } });
    21	  else io.stderr.write(`baton ${cmd}: ${msg}\n`);
    22	  return 2;
    23	}
    24	
    25	/**
    26	 * Repo-root discovery (plan §Root discovery): `--root` wins; else the nearest
    27	 * ancestor of io.cwd carrying `.handoff/` or `baton.config.json`; else the
    28	 * nearest ancestor carrying `.git` (the repo toplevel — and a boundary the
    29	 * walk never crosses, so a nested repo resolves to itself); else io.cwd.
    30	 * @param {any} io @param {Record<string, string | boolean>} flags
    31	 * @returns {string}
    32	 */
    33	export function resolveRoot(io, flags) {
    34	  if (typeof flags.root === 'string' && flags.root.length > 0) return flags.root;
    35	  // Normalize separators up front (gate-2 iter-2 M2): a Windows cwd arrives
    36	  // backslash-separated, but Node fs accepts forward slashes, so the ascent
    37	  // and the marker checks work in one forward-slash space. The last separator
    38	  // is either kind; the loop stops at a drive/UNC or filesystem root.
    39	  const start = String(io.cwd).replace(/\\/g, '/');
    40	  let dir = start;
    41	  while (true) {
    42	    try {
    43	      if (io.fs.existsSync(`${dir}/.handoff`) || io.fs.existsSync(`${dir}/baton.config.json`)) return dir;
    44	      if (io.fs.existsSync(`${dir}/.git`)) return dir; // repo toplevel; never walk past a .git boundary
    45	    } catch {
    46	      break;
    47	    }
    48	    const cut = dir.lastIndexOf('/');
    49	    if (cut < 0) break;
    50	    // A drive-relative root ("C:/") or the filesystem root ("/") is the ceiling.
    51	    const parent = cut === 0 ? '/' : dir.slice(0, cut);
    52	    if (parent === dir || /^[A-Za-z]:$/.test(parent)) break;
    53	    dir = parent;
    54	  }
    55	  return start;
    56	}
    57	
    58	/**
    59	 * Minimal flag parser: `--key value` pairs (value = next token not starting
    60	 * with --), bare `--key` booleans, positionals collected in order.
    61	 * @param {string[]} args
    62	 * @returns {{flags: Record<string, string | boolean>, positionals: string[]}}
    63	 */
    64	export function parseFlags(args) {
    65	  /** @type {Record<string, string | boolean>} */
    66	  const flags = {};
    67	  /** @type {string[]} */
    68	  const positionals = [];
    69	  for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i += 1) {
    70	    const a = args[i];
    71	    if (a.startsWith('--')) {
    72	      const key = a.slice(2);
    73	      const next = args[i + 1];
    74	      if (next !== undefined && !next.startsWith('--')) {
    75	        flags[key] = next;
    76	        i += 1;
    77	      } else {
    78	        flags[key] = true;
    79	      }
    80	    } else {
    81	      positionals.push(a);
    82	    }
    83	  }
    84	  return { flags, positionals };
    85	}
     1	// Single Claude Code hook entrypoint. hooks.json routes every event here with
     2	// the event name as argv[0]; this script's only job is to reach the core CLI
     3	// (never re-implement it) and to stay fail-open: a hook must never break the
     4	// host session, so every path returns 0.
     5	
     6	import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
     7	import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
     8	import * as nodeFs from 'node:fs';
     9	import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
    10	import { checkHandoffTree } from '../../../core/src/util/jail.mjs';
    11	
    12	/** @param {any} io */
    13	const batonBin = (io) => `${io.env?.CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT ?? '.'}/core/bin/baton.mjs`;
    14	
    15	/**
    16	 * execFile-shaped spawn that supports {input}: async execFile has no input
    17	 * option, and a child reading stdin would otherwise block forever (gate-2
    18	 * reviewer-b finding 1). The io contract for this adapter is therefore
    19	 * "promise execFile honoring opts.input as the child's stdin".
    20	 * @param {string} cmd @param {string[]} args @param {{cwd?: string, input?: string, timeout?: number}} [opts]
    21	 * @returns {Promise<{stdout: string, stderr: string}>}
    22	 */
    23	function execFileWithInput(cmd, args = [], opts = {}) {
    24	  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    25	    const child = spawn(cmd, args, { cwd: opts.cwd, timeout: opts.timeout ?? 25_000 });
    26	    let stdout = '';
    27	    let stderr = '';
    28	    child.stdout.on('data', (d) => (stdout += d));
    29	    child.stderr.on('data', (d) => (stderr += d));
    30	    child.on('error', reject);
    31	    child.on('close', (code) => {
    32	      if (code === 0) return resolve({ stdout, stderr });
    33	      const e = Object.assign(new Error(`Command failed: ${cmd}`), { code, stdout, stderr });
    34	      reject(e);
    35	    });
    36	    if (typeof opts.input === 'string' && child.stdin) child.stdin.write(opts.input);
    37	    child.stdin?.end();
    38	  });
    39	}
    40	
    41	// Hook-error diagnostics (gate-2 minor 16 / plan §Transcript policy log
    42	// contract): metadata-only entries — never the hook payload — with bounded
    43	// retention (last 50 entries / 7 days). The log rides .handoff/ so the purge
    44	// walk covers it.
    45	const HOOK_LOG_MAX = 50;
    46	const HOOK_LOG_MAX_AGE_MS = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
    47	
    48	/** @param {any} io @param {string} event @param {any} err */
    49	function logHookError(io, event, err) {
    50	  try {
    51	    // The diagnostics log rides .handoff/, so it passes the same managed-tree
    52	    // jail as every other write (threat model): refuse to create or write
    53	    // through a symlinked/escaped .handoff. Diagnostics must never be the hole
    54	    // the jail closes elsewhere — and this stays fail-open (skip, don't throw).
    55	    if (!checkHandoffTree(io.cwd, io).ok) return;
    56	    const dir = `${io.cwd}/.handoff/log`;
    57	    io.fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
    58	    const path = `${dir}/hook-errors.jsonl`;
    59	    const nowIso = typeof io.now === 'function' ? io.now() : new Date().toISOString();
    60	    /** @type {any[]} */
    61	    let entries = [];
    62	    try {
    63	      entries = io.fs
    64	        .readFileSync(path, 'utf8')
    65	        .split('\n')
    66	        .filter((/** @type {string} */ l) => l.trim() !== '')
    67	        .map((/** @type {string} */ l) => {
    68	          try {
    69	            return JSON.parse(l);
    70	          } catch {
    71	            return null;
    72	          }
    73	        })
    74	        .filter(Boolean);
    75	    } catch {
    76	      entries = [];
    77	    }
    78	    entries.push({ ts: nowIso, event, code: err?.code ?? null });
    79	    const cutoff = Date.parse(nowIso) - HOOK_LOG_MAX_AGE_MS;
    80	    entries = entries.filter((e) => typeof e.ts === 'string' && Date.parse(e.ts) >= cutoff).slice(-HOOK_LOG_MAX);
    81	    io.fs.writeFileSync(path, entries.map((e) => JSON.stringify(e)).join('\n') + '\n');
    82	  } catch {
    83	    // diagnostics must never break fail-open
    84	  }
    85	}
    86	
    87	/**
    88	 * Run a core baton command as a child process, forwarding the raw hook payload
    89	 * as the child's stdin. Rejections are swallowed (fail-open) — core commands
    90	 * carry their own hook-safety, and Claude ignores hook exit codes anyway —
    91	 * but each failure leaves a metadata-only diagnostic in .handoff/log/.
    92	 * @param {any} io @param {string[]} argv @param {string} event
    93	 */
    94	async function baton(io, argv, event) {
    95	  try {
    96	    await io.execFile('node', [batonBin(io), ...argv], { input: io.stdin });
    97	  } catch (err) {
    98	    // fail-open: a failing child must not break the session
    99	    logHookError(io, event, err);
   100	  }
   101	}
   102	
   103	/** @param {any} io @returns {any | null} parsed hook payload, or null */
   104	function parsePayload(io) {
   105	  try {
   106	    return JSON.parse(typeof io.stdin === 'string' ? io.stdin : '');
   107	  } catch {
   108	    return null;
   109	  }
   110	}
   111	
   112	/**
   113	 * SessionStart: not a checkpoint. If a pending bundle from ANOTHER platform
   114	 * exists (sealed, or open-but-limit-hit — the unsealed limit death), inject a
   115	 * receive suggestion via the Claude context shape. Own-platform bundles are
   116	 * this session's own work; quiet no-op.
   117	 * @param {any} io
   118	 */
   119	function sessionStart(io) {
   120	  let bundle;
   121	  try {
   122	    bundle = JSON.parse(io.fs.readFileSync(`${io.cwd}/.handoff/bundle.json`, 'utf8'));
   123	  } catch {
   124	    return 0;
   125	  }
   126	  const origin = bundle?.origin?.platform;
   127	  const pending = bundle?.handoff?.status === 'sealed' || bundle?.handoff?.reasonClass === 'usage-limit';
   128	  const foreign = typeof origin === 'string' && origin !== 'claude-code';
   129	  if (pending && foreign) {
   130	    io.stdout.write(
   131	      JSON.stringify({
   132	        hookSpecificOutput: {
   133	          hookEventName: 'SessionStart',
   134	          additionalContext: `A handoff bundle from ${origin} is pending in .handoff/ (${bundle.handoff.status === 'sealed' ? 'sealed' : 'limit-hit before sealing'}). Suggest running /baton:receive to resume that task with remapped roles.`,
   135	        },
   136	      }) + '\n',
   137	    );
   138	  }
   139	  return 0;
   140	}
   141	
   142	/**
   143	 * @param {string[]} args argv after the script path; args[0] is the hook event
   144	 * @param {any} io injected {cwd, env, stdin, stdout, stderr, fs, execFile, now, …}
   145	 * @returns {Promise<number>} always 0 — fail-open by construction
   146	 */
   147	export async function runHook(args, io) {
   148	  try {
   149	    const event = args[0] ?? 'unknown';
   150	
   151	    if (event === 'SessionStart') return sessionStart(io);
   152	
   153	    if (event === 'StopFailure') {
   154	      const errorType = parsePayload(io)?.error?.type;
   155	      const detectArgs = ['detect', '--platform', 'claude-code'];
   156	      if (typeof errorType === 'string') detectArgs.push('--structured-error-type', errorType);
   157	      await baton(io, detectArgs, event);
   158	      await baton(io, ['checkpoint', '--platform', 'claude-code'], event);
   159	      return 0;
   160	    }
   161	
   162	    // Stop / PreCompact / SessionEnd — and any future event — are routine
   163	    // mechanical checkpoints; core normalize degrades unknown payloads safely.
   164	    await baton(io, ['checkpoint', '--platform', 'claude-code'], event);
   165	    return 0;
   166	  } catch {
   167	    return 0;
   168	  }
   169	}
   170	
   171	// Main entry: hooks.json executes this file directly (`node …/hook.mjs <Event>`),
   172	// so a real invocation must build the real io and run — an export alone is a
   173	// production no-op (gate-2 reviewer-b finding 1).
   174	const isMain = (() => {
   175	  try {
   176	    return process.argv[1] ? import.meta.url === pathToFileURL(process.argv[1]).href : false;
   177	  } catch {
   178	    return false;
   179	  }
   180	})();

    11	// Snapshot rewrite throttle: journal append ALWAYS; the snapshot (and
    12	// HANDOFF.md) re-render only on an important event type, >30 s since the last

    14	const ROUTINE_TYPES = new Set(['note', 'file.touch']);

    18	// Transcript tail bounds (plan §Transcript policy): last 10 messages, 8 KB cap,
    19	// opt-in via baton.config.json capture.transcriptTail, PreCompact only.
    20	const TAIL_MESSAGES = 10;
    21	const TAIL_BYTES = 8192;
    22	
    23	/**
    24	 * Opt-in transcript-tail capture (gate-2 fix 10). Returns the bounded,
    25	 * redacted tail or null when the gate is closed: config off (the default),
    26	 * not a PreCompact payload, no usable transcript_path, or an unreadable /
    27	 * symlinked file. The path comes from an untrusted hook payload — it is read
    28	 * only under explicit opt-in, must lstat as a regular file, and its content
    29	 * passes the secret-redaction filter before storage.
    30	 * @param {string} root @param {any} raw parsed hook payload @param {any} io
    31	 * @returns {string | null}
    32	 */
    33	function captureTranscriptTail(root, raw, io) {
    34	  const cfg = safeReadJson(io.fs, `${root}/baton.config.json`);
    35	  if (!cfg.ok || cfg.value?.capture?.transcriptTail !== true) return null;
    36	
    37	  const eventName = raw && typeof raw === 'object' ? (raw.hook_event_name ?? raw.event) : null;
    38	  if (typeof eventName !== 'string' || !/precompact/i.test(eventName)) return null;
    39	  const path = raw.transcript_path;
    40	  if (typeof path !== 'string' || path.length === 0) return null;
    41	
    42	  try {
    43	    if (!io.fs.lstatSync(path).isFile()) return null;
    44	    // Allowlist the transcript path (gate-2 iter-2 m1): the payload is
    45	    // untrusted, so its resolved realpath must sit under the repo root, the
    46	    // user's ~/.claude tree (where Claude Code actually writes transcripts),
    47	    // or a configured capture.transcriptDir — never an arbitrary readable file.
    48	    const fileReal = io.fs.realpathSync(path);
    49	    /** @type {string[]} */
    50	    const allowed = [root];
    51	    const home = io.env?.HOME;
    52	    if (typeof home === 'string' && home.length > 0) allowed.push(`${home}/.claude`);
    53	    const dir = cfg.value?.capture?.transcriptDir;
    54	    if (typeof dir === 'string' && dir.length > 0) allowed.push(dir);
    55	    const under = (/** @type {string} */ base) => {
    56	      let baseReal;
    57	      try {
    58	        baseReal = io.fs.realpathSync(base);
    59	      } catch {
    60	        baseReal = base;
    61	      }
    62	      return fileReal === baseReal || fileReal.startsWith(`${baseReal}/`);
    63	    };
    64	    if (!allowed.some(under)) return null;
    65	
    66	    const text = io.fs.readFileSync(path, 'utf8');
    67	    const lines = text.split('\n').filter((/** @type {string} */ l) => l.trim() !== '');
    68	    let tail = lines.slice(-TAIL_MESSAGES).join('\n');
    69	    if (Buffer.byteLength(tail) > TAIL_BYTES) {
    70	      tail = Buffer.from(tail).subarray(-TAIL_BYTES).toString('utf8');
    71	    }
    72	    return redactSecrets(tail);
    73	  } catch {
    74	    return null; // unreadable transcript is never a checkpoint failure
    75	  }
    76	}
    77	
    78	/**
    79	 * `baton checkpoint` — mechanical checkpoint from a hook payload on stdin.
    80	 * Hook-safety rule: exit 0 on every soft failure so a checkpoint can never
    81	 * break the host harness; `--strict` opts into hard failures (CI).
    82	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io
    83	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
    84	 */
    85	export async function cmdCheckpoint(args, io) {
    86	  const { flags } = parseFlags(args);
    87	  const strict = flags.strict === true;
    88	  const platform = typeof flags.platform === 'string' ? flags.platform : null;
    89	  if (!platform) return usageError(io, flags, 'checkpoint', '--platform <claude-code|codex|cursor> is required');
    90	
    91	  try {
    92	    return await run(flags, platform, io);
    93	  } catch (err) {
    94	    const msg = /** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? String(err);
    95	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'checkpoint-failed', msg } });
    96	    io.stderr.write(`baton checkpoint: ${msg}\n`);
    97	    return strict ? 1 : 0;
    98	  }
    99	}
   100	
   101	/**
   102	 * @param {Record<string, string | boolean>} flags
   103	 * @param {string} platform
   104	 * @param {any} io
   105	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
   106	 */
   107	async function run(flags, platform, io) {
   108	  const strict = flags.strict === true;
   109	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
   110	  const paths = bundlePaths(root);
   111	  // The --json contract (gate-2 fix 11): exactly one envelope on stdout for
   112	  // every exit path — the exit code stays governed by hook-safety.
   113	  const finish = (/** @type {any} */ env, /** @type {number} */ code) => {
   114	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, env);
   115	    return code;
   116	  };
   117	
   118	  /** @type {any} */
   119	  let raw;
   120	  try {
   121	    raw = JSON.parse(typeof io.stdin === 'string' ? io.stdin : '');
   122	  } catch {
   123	    io.stderr.write('baton checkpoint: stdin could not be parsed as JSON — input ignored\n');
   124	    return finish({ ok: false, error: { code: 'bad-stdin', msg: 'stdin could not be parsed as JSON' } }, strict ? 1 : 0);
   125	  }
   126	
   127	  const session = { host: io.host, pid: io.pid, startTime: io.startTime };
   128	  const events = normalizeHookPayload(raw, platform, session);
   129	  if (events.length === 0) return finish({ ok: true, data: { events: 0, rewritten: false } }, 0);
   130	
   131	  // Opt-in transcript tail (gate-2 fix 10): rides the journal as its own
   132	  // IMPORTANT event so the snapshot rewrite below persists it, and purge can
   133	  // strip it from every retained copy by its property name.
   134	  const tail = captureTranscriptTail(root, raw, io);
   135	  if (tail !== null) {
   136	    events.push({
   137	      type: 'transcript.set',
   138	      payload: { transcript: { capturedAt: io.now(), tail } },
   139	      source: platform,
   140	      sessionHint: events[0]?.sessionHint,
   141	      unstable: events[0]?.unstable ?? true,
   142	    });
   143	  }

   146	  for (const w of warnings) io.stderr.write(`baton checkpoint: ${w}\n`);
   147	
   148	  // An unsafe managed tree (symlinked .handoff — gate-2 fix 6) is NOT "no
   149	  // bundle yet": seeding here would write through the link. Refuse everything;
   150	  // hook-safety keeps the refusal soft outside --strict.
   151	  if (unsafe === true) {
   152	    io.stderr.write('baton checkpoint: managed tree failed the symlink/realpath jail — nothing written\n');
   153	    return finish({ ok: false, error: { code: 'unsafe-tree', msg: warnings[0] ?? 'managed tree failed the jail check' } }, strict ? 1 : 0);
   154	  }
   155	
   156	  // Opt-in debounce (gate-2 major 14; Cursor afterFileEdit fires per edit):
   157	  // at most one real checkpoint per window, tracked by a per-platform stamp.
   158	  // The read-and-set runs UNDER the repo lock (gate-2 iter-2 M5) so two
   159	  // simultaneous afterFileEdit hooks can't both observe no stamp and both
   160	  // checkpoint — exactly one wins the window.
   161	  if (flags.debounce !== undefined) {
   162	    const seconds = Number(flags.debounce);
   163	    if (!Number.isFinite(seconds) || seconds <= 0) return usageError(io, flags, 'checkpoint', '--debounce <seconds> must be a positive number');
   164	    const stampPath = `${paths.logDir}/debounce-${platform}.json`;
   165	    const proceed = withLock(root, io, () => {
   166	      const stamp = safeReadJson(io.fs, stampPath);
   167	      if (stamp.ok && typeof stamp.value?.at === 'string' && Date.parse(io.now()) - Date.parse(stamp.value.at) < seconds * 1000) {
   168	        return false;
   169	      }
   170	      ensureDir(io.fs, paths.logDir);
   171	      atomicWriteJson(io.fs, stampPath, { at: io.now() });
   172	      return true;
   173	    });
   174	    if (!proceed) return finish({ ok: true, data: { events: 0, rewritten: false, debounced: true } }, 0);
   175	  }
   176	
   177	  let active = bundle;
   178	  let mustRewrite = false;
   179	
   180	  // Semantic isolation (plan §Concurrency): a stable foreign session or a
   181	  // mismatched origin platform must not merge into the active bundle. Checked
   182	  // before ANY write so a rejection leaves the whole tree untouched.
   183	  const originHint = active?.origin?.sessionHint;
   184	  const hasStableOwner = active !== null && typeof originHint === 'string' && originHint.length > 0 && active.origin.unstable !== true;
   185	  const stableIncoming = events.find(
   186	    (/** @type {any} */ e) => typeof e.sessionHint === 'string' && e.sessionHint.length > 0 && e.unstable === false,
   187	  );
   188	  const unstableIncoming = events.some((/** @type {any} */ e) => e.unstable === true);
   189	
   190	  if (hasStableOwner && stableIncoming) {
   191	    const foreign = stableIncoming.sessionHint !== originHint || platform !== active.origin.platform;
   192	    if (foreign && flags['take-over'] !== true) {
   193	      io.stderr.write(
   194	        `baton checkpoint: event from a foreign session/origin (${platform}/${stableIncoming.sessionHint} vs active ${active.origin.platform}/${originHint}) — rejected to keep sessions isolated; rerun with --take-over to archive the active bundle and start fresh\n`,
   195	      );
   196	      return finish({ ok: false, error: { code: 'foreign-session', msg: 'event from a foreign session/origin rejected; rerun with --take-over' } }, 0);
   197	    }
   198	    if (foreign) {
   199	      rotateJournal(root, 'takeover', io);
   200	      const fresh = emptyBundle(
   201	        { platform, model: typeof flags.model === 'string' ? flags.model : 'unknown', goal: active.task?.goal ?? 'unknown' },
   202	        io.now(),
   203	      );
   204	      fresh.origin.sessionHint = stableIncoming.sessionHint;
   205	      writeSnapshot(root, fresh, io);
   206	      io.stderr.write(`baton checkpoint: took over — prior bundle archived to history/, fresh bundle owned by ${platform}/${stableIncoming.sessionHint}\n`);
   207	      active = fresh;
   208	      mustRewrite = true;
   209	    }
   210	  }
   211	
   212	  if (unstableIncoming) {
   213	    io.stderr.write('baton checkpoint: event carries an unstable (generated) session hint — ownership unverifiable; applied with this warning only\n');
   214	  }
   215	
   216	  if (active === null) {
   217	    active = emptyBundle(
   218	      { platform, model: typeof flags.model === 'string' ? flags.model : 'unknown', goal: 'unknown' },
   219	      io.now(),
   220	    );
   221	    writeSnapshot(root, active, io);
   222	    // Self-applying seed event (gate-2 iter-2 B6): a journal-only rebuild
   223	    // (snapshot + .bak both lost) must restore the bundle's identity, not the
   224	    // "unknown" placeholder resolveBase seeds. bundle.seed carries bundleId,
   225	    // origin, task, and generation and is applied on replay.
   226	    appendJournal(
   227	      root,
   228	      {
   229	        ts: io.now(),
   230	        type: 'bundle.seed',
   231	        payload: {
   232	          bundleId: active.bundleId,
   233	          generation: active.generation,
   234	          createdAt: active.createdAt,
   235	          origin: active.origin,
   236	          task: { goal: active.task.goal },
   237	          text: `[seed] auto-seeded a new bundle on ${platform} (model ${active.origin.model})`,
   238	        },
   239	        writerId: [platform, io.pid, 'seed'].join('-'),
   240	        dedupeKey: dedupeKey({ ts: io.now(), type: 'bundle.seed', bundleId: active.bundleId }),
   241	      },
   242	      io,
   243	    );
   244	    io.stderr.write('baton checkpoint: no bundle found — auto-seeded a new one at .handoff/bundle.json\n');
   245	    mustRewrite = true;
   246	  }
   247	
   248	  // Ownership adoption (gate-2 fix 2): a bundle whose owner is unverified — a
   249	  // fresh seed (null hint) or a receive that defaulted to an unstable hint —
   250	  // adopts the first STABLE same-platform session as its owner, so semantic
   251	  // isolation engages from then on instead of never.
   252	  const ownerUnverified = active.origin.sessionHint === null || active.origin.unstable === true;
   253	  if (ownerUnverified && stableIncoming && active.origin.platform === platform) {
   254	    active = { ...active, origin: { ...active.origin, sessionHint: stableIncoming.sessionHint, unstable: false } };
   255	    writeSnapshot(root, active, io);
   256	    io.stderr.write(`baton checkpoint: adopted ${platform}/${stableIncoming.sessionHint} as the bundle owner (first verified session)\n`);
   257	    mustRewrite = true;
   258	  }
   259	
   260	  let lastSeq = 0;
   261	  for (const ev of events) {
   262	    const ts = typeof ev.ts === 'string' ? ev.ts : io.now();
   263	    const entry = {
   264	      ...ev,
   265	      ts,
   266	      dedupeKey:
   267	        typeof ev.dedupeKey === 'string' && ev.dedupeKey.length > 0
   268	          ? ev.dedupeKey
   269	          : dedupeKey({ ts, type: ev.type, payload: ev.payload, source: ev.source, sessionHint: ev.sessionHint }),
   270	      writerId: [platform, io.pid, ev.sessionHint ?? 'local'].join('-'),
   271	    };
   272	    lastSeq = appendJournal(root, entry, io);
   273	  }
   274	
   275	  const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, paths.snapshot);
   276	  const snapSeq = snap.ok && typeof snap.value?.journalSeq === 'number' ? snap.value.journalSeq : 0;
   277	  const snapUpdated = snap.ok && typeof snap.value?.updatedAt === 'string' ? Date.parse(snap.value.updatedAt) : 0;
   278	  const important = events.some((/** @type {any} */ e) => !ROUTINE_TYPES.has(e.type));
   279	  const elapsed = Date.parse(io.now()) - snapUpdated > THROTTLE_WINDOW_MS;
   280	  const accreted = lastSeq - snapSeq;
   281	
   282	  let rewritten = false;
   283	  if (mustRewrite || important || elapsed || accreted >= THROTTLE_EVENT_COUNT) {
   284	    const merged = loadBundle(root, io).bundle;
   285	    if (merged) {
   286	      // Bounded git refresh on EVERY snapshot rewrite (gate-2 iter-2 M1): a
   287	      // mechanical checkpoint is defined to carry branch/HEAD/dirty, so any
   288	      // rewrite — routine Stop that cleared the throttle included, not only
   289	      // "important" events — refreshes git; unavailability degrades to the
   290	      // previous git state (or null), never a failure.
   291	      const git = await gitSnapshot({ execFile: io.execFile, cwd: root, fs: io.fs });
   292	      if (git !== null) merged.git = git;
   293	      writeSnapshot(root, merged, io);
   294	      rewritten = true;
   295	    }
   296	  }
   297	  return finish({ ok: true, data: { events: events.length, rewritten } }, 0);
   298	}

     2	import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
     3	import { mkdtempSync, rmSync, writeFileSync, readFileSync, existsSync, mkdirSync, readdirSync } from 'node:fs';
     4	import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
     5	import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
     6	import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';

     9	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    10	// Gate-2 iteration-2 findings M7 (reviewer-a #13) + M5 (reviewer-a #10):
    11	// genuinely CONCURRENT acceptance, not sequential. Two commit processes are
    12	// released from one filesystem barrier against a single prepared receipt:
    13	// exactly one transition must land (one generation bump, one receive_log
    14	// entry), the loser rejected with state untouched. And two debounced
    15	// afterFileEdit checkpoints released together must yield exactly one append.
    16	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    17	
    18	const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
    19	const repoRoot = join(here, '..', '..');
    20	const BATON = join(repoRoot, 'core', 'bin', 'baton.mjs');
    21	const CHILD_TIMEOUT_MS = 30_000;
    22	
    23	/** @type {string[]} */
    24	const dirs = [];
    25	const scratch = (prefix) => {
    26	  const d = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), prefix));
    27	  dirs.push(d);
    28	  return d;
    29	};
    30	after(() => {
    31	  for (const d of dirs) rmSync(d, { recursive: true, force: true });
    32	});
    33	
    34	const spawned = (script, args) =>
    35	  new Promise((resolve) => {
    36	    const child = spawn('node', [script, ...args]);
    37	    let stderr = '';
    38	    let stdout = '';
    39	    child.stdout.on('data', (d) => (stdout += d));
    40	    child.stderr.on('data', (d) => (stderr += d));
    41	    const killer = setTimeout(() => child.kill('SIGKILL'), CHILD_TIMEOUT_MS);
    42	    child.on('close', (code, signal) => {
    43	      clearTimeout(killer);
    44	      resolve({ code, signal, stdout, stderr });
    45	    });
    46	  });
    47	
    48	async function until(pred, ms = 15_000) {
    49	  const t0 = Date.now();
    50	  while (!pred()) {
    51	    if (Date.now() - t0 > ms) throw new Error('barrier timeout');
    52	    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 10));
    53	  }
    54	}
    55	
    56	const NOW = '2026-07-12T00:00:00.000Z';
    57	const sealed = () => ({
    58	  schema: 'baton/bundle@1',
    59	  bundleId: 'b_recvconc000000',
    60	  generation: 1,
    61	  createdAt: NOW,
    62	  updatedAt: NOW,
    63	  origin: { platform: 'claude-code', model: 'claude-fable-5', sessionHint: 's', unstable: false },
    64	  task: { goal: 'ship', constraints: [], acceptance: [] },
    65	  plan: { steps: [] },
    66	  decisions: [],
    67	  files: { touched: [] },
    68	  roles: { assignments: {} },
    69	  git: null,
    70	  handoff: { status: 'sealed', reason: 'limits', reasonClass: 'usage-limit', toPlatformHint: null, finalizedAt: NOW, receive_log: [] },
    71	  journalSeq: 0,
    72	  compaction: { droppedDecisions: 0, note: null },
    73	  dedupeRing: [],
    74	});
    75	
    76	describe('M7 — two INDEPENDENTLY-prepared receivers race to commit: exactly one wins', () => {
    77	  it('one generation bump, one receive archive, loser rejected with state untouched', async () => {
    78	    const root = scratch('baton-recvconc-');
    79	    const barrier = scratch('baton-recvconc-barrier-');
    80	    mkdirSync(join(root, '.handoff'), { recursive: true });
    81	    writeFileSync(join(root, '.handoff', 'bundle.json'), JSON.stringify(sealed(), null, 2) + '\n');
    82	
    83	    // Each child runs its OWN prepare (against the same sealed state) and then
    84	    // its own commit — the faithful competing-receivers race, not a shared
    85	    // single receipt double-committed. Both prepares complete before the
    86	    // barrier; the barrier releases both COMMITs together. The winner bumps the
    87	    // generation/receive_log, so the loser's re-derivation under the lock no
    88	    // longer matches its token → stale-receipt rejection, zero mutation.
    89	    const receiver = `
    90	import { writeFileSync, existsSync } from 'node:fs';
    91	import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
    92	const [bin, root, idx, barrierDir] = process.argv.slice(2);
    93	const args = ['--platform', 'codex', '--origin', 'claude-code', '--reason', 'limits', '--root', root];
    94	const prep = spawnSync('node', [bin, 'receive', '--json', ...args], { encoding: 'utf8' });
    95	if (prep.status !== 0) { writeFileSync(barrierDir + '/prep-fail-' + idx, prep.stderr || ''); process.exit(2); }
    96	const token = JSON.parse(prep.stdout.trim().split('\\n').pop()).data.token;
    97	writeFileSync(barrierDir + '/ready-' + idx, '');
    98	while (!existsSync(barrierDir + '/go')) { /* barrier: both prepared, race the commit */ }
    99	const r = spawnSync('node', [bin, 'receive', '--commit', token, ...args], { encoding: 'utf8' });
   100	writeFileSync(barrierDir + '/done-' + idx, JSON.stringify({ status: r.status }));
   101	process.exit(r.status ?? 1);
   102	`;
   103	    const receiverPath = join(barrier, 'receiver.mjs');
   104	    writeFileSync(receiverPath, receiver);
   105	
   106	    const kids = [1, 2].map((i) => spawned(receiverPath, [BATON, root, String(i), barrier]));
   107	    await until(() => existsSync(join(barrier, 'ready-1')) && existsSync(join(barrier, 'ready-2')));
   108	    writeFileSync(join(barrier, 'go'), '');
   109	    const results = await Promise.all(kids);
   110	
   111	    const codes = results.map((r) => r.code).sort();
   112	    assert.deepEqual(codes, [0, 1], `exactly one commit succeeds and one is rejected; got ${JSON.stringify(codes)} (stderr: ${results.map((r) => r.stderr).join(' | ')})`);
   113	
   114	    const bundle = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(root, '.handoff', 'bundle.json'), 'utf8'));
   115	    assert.equal(bundle.generation, 2, 'exactly one generation bump (no double transition)');
   116	    assert.equal(bundle.handoff.status, 'open', 'the winner opened a fresh writable generation');
   117	    assert.equal(bundle.origin.platform, 'codex', 'the winning receiver adopted ownership');
   118	    // The archived received seal carries exactly one receive_log entry.
   119	    const historyDir = join(root, '.handoff', 'history');
   120	    const freezes = existsSync(historyDir) ? readdirSync(historyDir).filter((n) => n.endsWith('.receive.json')) : [];
   121	    assert.equal(freezes.length, 1, 'exactly one receive archive was frozen to history');
   122	  });
   123	});
   124	
   125	describe('M5 — two debounced afterFileEdit checkpoints race: exactly one appends', () => {
   126	  it('the second, inside the window, is debounced away under the lock', async () => {
   127	    const root = scratch('baton-debounce-conc-');
   128	    const barrier = scratch('baton-debounce-conc-barrier-');
   129	
   130	    const writer = `
   131	import { writeFileSync, existsSync } from 'node:fs';
   132	import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
   133	const [bin, root, idx, barrierDir] = process.argv.slice(2);
   134	const payload = JSON.stringify({ event: 'afterFileEdit', schema: 'baton/event@1', type: 'decision', payload: { summary: 'edit-' + idx }, sessionHint: 'deb-sess', unstable: false });
   135	writeFileSync(barrierDir + '/ready-' + idx, '');
   136	while (!existsSync(barrierDir + '/go')) { /* barrier */ }
   137	const r = spawnSync('node', [bin, 'checkpoint', '--platform', 'cursor', '--debounce', '120', '--root', root], { input: payload, encoding: 'utf8' });
   138	writeFileSync(barrierDir + '/done-' + idx, JSON.stringify({ status: r.status }));
   139	process.exit(r.status ?? 1);
   140	`;
   141	    const writerPath = join(barrier, 'writer.mjs');
   142	    writeFileSync(writerPath, writer);
   143	
   144	    const kids = [1, 2].map((i) => spawned(writerPath, [BATON, root, String(i), barrier]));
   145	    await until(() => existsSync(join(barrier, 'ready-1')) && existsSync(join(barrier, 'ready-2')));
   146	    writeFileSync(join(barrier, 'go'), '');
   147	    const results = await Promise.all(kids);
   148	    for (const r of results) assert.equal(r.code, 0, `both exit 0 (hook-safe); signal ${r.signal}, stderr ${r.stderr}`);
   149	
   150	    const journal = readFileSync(join(root, '.handoff', 'journal.ndjson'), 'utf8')
   151	      .split('\n')
   152	      .filter((l) => l.trim() !== '')
   153	      .map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
   154	    const decisions = journal.filter((e) => e.type === 'decision');
   155	    assert.equal(decisions.length, 1, `exactly one edit is checkpointed in the debounce window; got ${decisions.length}`);
   156	  });

     1	import { atomicWriteText, safeReadJson, ensureDir, atomicWriteJson } from '../util/fsx.mjs';
     2	import { appendEntry, readAllTolerant } from '../util/jsonl.mjs';
     3	import { checkHandoffTree, assertHandoffTreeSafe } from '../util/jail.mjs';
