    10	const RETAIN_PER_KIND = 10;
    11	
    12	/** @param {string} root */
    13	export function bundlePaths(root) {
    14	  const dir = `${root}/.handoff`;
    15	  return {
    16	    dir,
    17	    snapshot: `${dir}/bundle.json`,
    18	    bak: `${dir}/bundle.json.bak`,
    19	    journal: `${dir}/journal.ndjson`,
    20	    handoffMd: `${dir}/HANDOFF.md`,
    21	    historyDir: `${dir}/history`,
    22	    lockDir: `${dir}/lock`,
    23	    logDir: `${dir}/log`,
    24	  };
    25	}
    26	
    27	/** @param {string} iso */
    28	const tsForFile = (iso) => iso.replace(/[:.]/g, '-');
    29	
    30	/** @param {ReturnType<typeof bundlePaths>} p */
    31	const markerPath = (p) => `${p.dir}/rotation.marker.json`;
    32	
    33	/**
    34	 * Reconcile a leftover rotation marker (crash recovery, part of load). The
    35	 * mutations run under the repo lock, acquired WITHOUT waiting (gate-2 minor
    36	 * 16): a held lock means someone is mid-operation — reconciliation defers to
    37	 * the next unlocked load instead of writing behind their back.
    38	 * @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof bundlePaths>} p @param {string[]} warnings @param {string} root
    39	 */
    40	function reconcileMarker(io, p, warnings, root) {
    41	  const mp = markerPath(p);
    42	  if (!io.fs.existsSync(mp)) return;
    43	  try {
    44	    withLock(root, { ...io, lockRetry: { attempts: 0, delayMs: 0 } }, () => {
    45	      if (!io.fs.existsSync(mp)) return; // a competitor reconciled while we acquired
    46	      const marker = safeReadJson(io.fs, mp);
    47	      if (!marker.ok) {
    48	        io.fs.unlinkSync(mp);
    49	        warnings.push('rotation marker was unparseable — rolled the rotation back (marker cleared, journal preserved)');
    50	        return;
    51	      }
    52	      const stem = `${tsForFile(marker.value.startedAt)}.${marker.value.kind}`;
    53	      const freeze = `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.json`;
    54	      const rotated = `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.ndjson`;
    55	
    56	      if (io.fs.existsSync(freeze)) {
    57	        // Roll forward: finish whichever step the crash interrupted.
    58	        if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal) && !io.fs.existsSync(rotated)) {
    59	          io.fs.renameSync(p.journal, rotated);
    60	        }
    61	        if (!io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
    62	          atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.journal, '');
    63	        }
    64	        io.fs.unlinkSync(mp);
    65	        warnings.push(`completed an interrupted rotation (roll-forward of ${stem}); marker cleared`);
    66	      } else {
    67	        io.fs.unlinkSync(mp);
    68	        warnings.push(`aborted an incomplete rotation (roll-back; ${stem} freeze never landed); marker cleared, journal preserved`);
    69	      }
    70	    });
    71	  } catch (err) {
    72	    if (err instanceof LockHeldError) {
    73	      warnings.push('a rotation marker is present but the repo lock is held — reconciliation deferred to the next unlocked load');
    74	      return;
    75	    }
    76	    throw err;
    77	  }
    78	}
    79	
    80	/**
    81	 * @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof bundlePaths>} p @param {string[]} warnings
    82	 * @returns {any | null} a usable base bundle, or null when nothing exists
    83	 */
    84	function resolveBase(io, p, warnings) {
    85	  const snapshotExists = io.fs.existsSync(p.snapshot);
    86	  if (snapshotExists) {
    87	    const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.snapshot);
    88	    if (snap.ok && validateBundle(snap.value).ok) return snap.value;
    89	    warnings.push(
    90	      snap.ok
    91	        ? 'snapshot bundle.json failed schema validation — attempting recovery'
    92	        : `snapshot bundle.json is unreadable (${snap.error}) — attempting recovery`,
    93	    );
    94	    const bak = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.bak);
    95	    if (bak.ok && validateBundle(bak.value).ok) {
    96	      warnings.push('recovered from backup bundle.json.bak; replaying journal past its journalSeq');
    97	      return bak.value;
    98	    }
    99	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.bak)) warnings.push('backup bundle.json.bak is also unusable');
   100	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
   101	      warnings.push('rebuilding the bundle by replaying the whole journal over a fresh seed (recovery of last resort)');
   102	      return emptyBundle({ platform: 'unknown', model: 'unknown', goal: 'unknown (rebuilt from journal)' }, io.now());
   103	    }
   104	    return null;
   105	  }
   106	  if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
   107	    warnings.push('no snapshot present — rebuilding the bundle from the journal');
   108	    return emptyBundle({ platform: 'unknown', model: 'unknown', goal: 'unknown (rebuilt from journal)' }, io.now());
   109	  }
   110	  return null;
   111	}
   112	
   113	/**
   114	 * Load the active bundle with full crash recovery. Read path — not lock-gated;
   115	 * rotation-marker reconciliation is part of load. A managed tree that fails
   116	 * the symlink/realpath jail (gate-2 fix 6) is refused outright: bundle null,
   117	 * `unsafe: true`, and the problem in warnings — callers must not treat this as
   118	 * "no bundle yet" and seed through the link.
   119	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
   120	 * @returns {{bundle: any | null, warnings: string[], unsafe?: boolean}}
   121	 */
   122	export function loadBundle(root, io) {
   123	  const p = bundlePaths(root);
   124	  /** @type {string[]} */
   125	  const warnings = [];
   126	  if (!io.fs.existsSync(p.dir)) return { bundle: null, warnings };
   127	
   128	  const safe = checkHandoffTree(root, io);
   129	  if (!safe.ok) return { bundle: null, warnings: [safe.problem], unsafe: true };
   130	
   131	  reconcileMarker(io, p, warnings, root);
   132	
   133	  // An interrupted purge is visible on every read path (gate-2 minor 16): the
   134	  // tree may still hold transcript copies until purge-transcript finishes.
   135	  if (io.fs.existsSync(`${p.dir}/purge.marker.json`)) {
   136	    warnings.push('an interrupted purge left its marker — run baton purge-transcript to finish scrubbing every retained copy');
   137	  }
   138	
   139	  let bundle = resolveBase(io, p, warnings);
   140	  if (bundle === null) return { bundle: null, warnings };
   141	
   142	  if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
   143	    const { entries, warnings: jw } = readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal);
   144	    for (const w of jw) {
   145	      warnings.push(`journal line ${w.line}: ${w.kind}${w.kind === 'seq-order' ? ` (prev ${w.prev}, got ${w.seq})` : ''}`);
   146	    }
   147	    for (const e of entries) {
   148	      // Envelope guard (gate-2 iter-2 B4): a parseable but non-object line
   149	      // (null, number, bare string) is not an event — skip it with a warning
   150	      // rather than crash replay on `e.seq` / `e.dedupeKey`.
   151	      if (e === null || typeof e !== 'object' || Array.isArray(e)) {
   152	        warnings.push('skipped a malformed journal line (not an event envelope)');
   153	        continue;
   154	      }
   155	      if (typeof e.seq === 'number' && e.seq <= bundle.journalSeq) continue;
   156	      bundle = applyEvent(bundle, e);
   157	    }
   158	  }
   159	  return { bundle, warnings };
   160	}
   161	
   162	/**
   163	 * Lock-context snapshot write: caller already holds the repo lock and passes
   164	 * its fencing token; the write is fence-checked immediately before landing
   165	 * (the fast-abort layer — gate-2 fix, reviewer-b finding 12).
   166	 * @param {string} root @param {any} bundle @param {any} io @param {string} token
   167	 */
   168	export function writeSnapshotIn(root, bundle, io, token) {
   169	  const p = bundlePaths(root);
   170	  return guardedWrite(root, io, token, () => {
   171	    assertHandoffTreeSafe(root, io);
   172	    ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
   173	    // Size discipline runs in the write path itself (gate-2 fix 10): every
   174	    // persisted snapshot is within budget; identity for in-budget bundles.
   175	    const bounded = compact(bundle);
   176	    // Validate-before-backup (gate-2 major 15): a corrupt current snapshot
   177	    // must never overwrite a good .bak — the backup only rotates when the
   178	    // outgoing snapshot itself would be recoverable.
   179	    const prev = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.snapshot);
   180	    if (prev.ok && validateBundle(prev.value).ok) {
   181	      atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.bak, io.fs.readFileSync(p.snapshot, 'utf8'));
   182	    }
   183	    atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.snapshot, JSON.stringify(bounded, null, 2) + '\n');
   184	    atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.handoffMd, renderHandoffMd(bounded));
   185	  });
   186	}
   187	
   188	/**
   189	 * Persist a snapshot: back up the previous good one, write atomically, render
   190	 * HANDOFF.md alongside. Mutation — runs under the repo lock.
   191	 * @param {string} root @param {any} bundle @param {any} io
   192	 */
   193	export function writeSnapshot(root, bundle, io) {
   194	  return withLock(root, io, (token) => writeSnapshotIn(root, bundle, io, token));
   195	}
   196	
   197	/**
   198	 * Append one journal entry under the repo lock. A pre-numbered entry keeps its
   199	 * seq; a seq-less entry is allocated max(snapshot.journalSeq, journal tail) + 1.
   200	 * @param {string} root @param {any} entry @param {any} io
   201	 * @returns {number} the entry's (possibly allocated) seq
   202	 */
   203	export function appendJournal(root, entry, io) {
   204	  const p = bundlePaths(root);
   205	  return withLock(root, io, (token) =>
   206	    guardedWrite(root, io, token, () => {
   207	      assertHandoffTreeSafe(root, io);
   208	      ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
   209	      let seq = entry.seq;
   210	      let toAppend = entry;
   211	      if (typeof seq !== 'number') {
   212	        const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.snapshot);
   213	        const snapSeq = snap.ok && typeof snap.value?.journalSeq === 'number' ? snap.value.journalSeq : 0;
   214	        let tailSeq = 0;
   215	        if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
   216	          for (const e of readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal).entries) {
   217	            if (typeof e.seq === 'number' && e.seq > tailSeq) tailSeq = e.seq;
   218	          }
   219	        }
   220	        seq = Math.max(snapSeq, tailSeq) + 1;
   221	        toAppend = { ...entry, seq };
   222	      }
   223	      appendEntry(io.fs, p.journal, toAppend);
   224	      return seq;
   225	    }),
   226	  );
   227	}
   228	
   229	/**
   230	 * Crash-safe journal rotation (marker → freeze → rename → fresh journal →
   231	 * clear marker), with 10-per-kind history retention. Mutation — lock-gated.
   232	 * @param {string} root @param {'finalize'|'takeover'|'receive'} kind @param {any} io
   233	 * @returns {string} the history stem for this rotation
   234	 */
   235	export function rotateJournal(root, kind, io) {
   236	  return withLock(root, io, (token) => rotateJournalIn(root, kind, io, token));
   237	}
   238	
   239	/**
   240	 * Lock-context rotation: caller holds the lock and passes its fencing token.
   241	 * @param {string} root @param {'finalize'|'takeover'|'receive'} kind @param {any} io @param {string} token
   242	 * @returns {string} the history stem for this rotation
   243	 */
   244	export function rotateJournalIn(root, kind, io, token) {
   245	  const p = bundlePaths(root);
   246	  return guardedWrite(root, io, token, () => {
   247	    assertHandoffTreeSafe(root, io);
   248	    ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
   249	    const startedAt = io.now();
   250	    const stem = `${tsForFile(startedAt)}.${kind}`;
   251	    const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.snapshot);
   252	    const seq = snap.ok && typeof snap.value?.journalSeq === 'number' ? snap.value.journalSeq : 0;
   253	
   254	    atomicWriteJson(io.fs, markerPath(p), { kind, startedAt, seq });
   255	    ensureDir(io.fs, p.historyDir);
   256	
   257	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.snapshot)) {
   258	      // Atomic freeze (gate-2 minor 16): tmp+rename, so a crash mid-copy can
   259	      // never leave a partial history snapshot that recovery would trust.
   260	      atomicWriteText(io.fs, `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.json`, io.fs.readFileSync(p.snapshot, 'utf8'));
   261	    } else {
   262	      atomicWriteText(io.fs, `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.json`, '');
   263	    }
   264	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
   265	      io.fs.renameSync(p.journal, `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.ndjson`);
   266	    } else {
   267	      atomicWriteText(io.fs, `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.ndjson`, '');
   268	    }
   269	    atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.journal, '');
   270	
   271	    // Retention: newest RETAIN_PER_KIND per kind (ISO-derived stems sort by time).
   272	    const freezes = io.fs
   273	      .readdirSync(p.historyDir)
   274	      .filter((/** @type {string} */ n) => n.endsWith(`.${kind}.json`))
   275	      .sort();
   276	    while (freezes.length > RETAIN_PER_KIND) {
   277	      const oldest = /** @type {string} */ (freezes.shift());
   278	      const oldStem = oldest.slice(0, -'.json'.length);
   279	      for (const suffix of ['.json', '.ndjson']) {
   280	        try {
   281	          io.fs.unlinkSync(`${p.historyDir}/${oldStem}${suffix}`);
   282	        } catch {
   283	          // already absent — retention is best-effort per file
   284	        }
   285	      }
   286	    }
   287	
   288	    io.fs.unlinkSync(markerPath(p));
   289	    return stem;
   290	  });
   291	}
     1	import { jailRelPath } from '../util/jail.mjs';
     2	
     3	const RING_CAP = 500;
     4	
     5	/** Audit note for a refused payload — visible in the decision log, never a throw. */
     6	const refusalNote = (/** @type {string} */ type, /** @type {any} */ detail) =>
     7	  `[${type} with invalid payload refused: ${JSON.stringify(detail).slice(0, 120)}]`;
     8	
     9	/**
    10	 * Pure event reducer. Returns a new bundle; never mutates the input.
    11	 * Idempotent: an event whose dedupeKey is still in the bundle's ring is a no-op.
    12	 * Unknown event types degrade to a decision-log note — a checkpoint must never
    13	 * fail because a newer adapter emitted something this core doesn't know — and
    14	 * journal payloads are untrusted (gate-2 fix 6): malformed shapes degrade to a
    15	 * refusal note and repository paths are jailed before storage.
    16	 * @param {any} bundle
    17	 * @param {{seq: number, ts: string, type: string, dedupeKey: string, writerId?: string, source?: string, payload: any}} event
    18	 */
    19	export function applyEvent(bundle, event) {
    20	  // Journal lines are untrusted (gate-2 iter-2 B4): a parseable null/number/
    21	  // string is a valid JSON line but not an event envelope — degrade to a
    22	  // no-op rather than crash replay. The store's load loop also pre-filters and
    23	  // warns; this is the last-line guard for any direct caller.
    24	  if (event === null || typeof event !== 'object' || Array.isArray(event)) return bundle;
    25	  if (bundle.dedupeRing.includes(event.dedupeKey)) return bundle;
    26	
    27	  const out = structuredClone(bundle);
    28	  const { seq, ts, type, payload = {} } = structuredClone(event);
    29	
    30	  switch (type) {
    31	    case 'decision': {
    32	      /** @type {{seq: number, ts: string, summary: any, detail?: any}} */
    33	      const entry = { seq, ts, summary: payload.summary };
    34	      if (payload.detail !== undefined) entry.detail = payload.detail;
    35	      out.decisions.push(entry);
    36	      break;
    37	    }
    38	    case 'note':
    39	      out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: payload.text });
    40	      // A structured StopFailure rate_limit note marks the open bundle
    41	      // limit-hit (gate-2 fix 3): the unsealed limit death must be visible to
    42	      // the next SessionStart on any platform, not buried in the decision log.
    43	      if (payload.structured?.kind === 'stop-failure' && payload.errorType === 'rate_limit' && out.handoff.status === 'open') {
    44	        out.handoff = { ...out.handoff, reason: out.handoff.reason ?? payload.text ?? null, reasonClass: 'usage-limit' };
    45	      }
    46	      break;
    47	    case 'plan.set':
    48	      if (Array.isArray(payload.steps) && payload.steps.every((/** @type {any} */ s) => s !== null && typeof s === 'object')) {
    49	        out.plan.steps = payload.steps;
    50	      } else {
    51	        out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: refusalNote('plan.set', payload?.steps) });
    52	      }
    53	      break;
    54	    case 'plan.step': {
    55	      if (payload === null || typeof payload !== 'object' || typeof payload.id !== 'string') {
    56	        out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: refusalNote('plan.step', payload) });
    57	        break;
    58	      }
    59	      const existing = out.plan.steps.find((/** @type {any} */ s) => s.id === payload.id);
    60	      if (existing) Object.assign(existing, payload);
    61	      else out.plan.steps.push(payload);
    62	      break;
    63	    }
    64	    case 'file.touch': {
    65	      const safePath = jailRelPath(payload?.path);
    66	      if (safePath === null) {
    67	        out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: refusalNote('file.touch', payload?.path) });
    68	        break;
    69	      }
    70	      const entry = { path: safePath, op: typeof payload.op === 'string' ? payload.op : 'touch', lastTs: ts };
    71	      const i = out.files.touched.findIndex((/** @type {any} */ f) => f.path === safePath);
    72	      if (i === -1) out.files.touched.push(entry);
    73	      else out.files.touched[i] = entry;
    74	      break;
    75	    }
    76	    case 'roles.remap':
    77	      if (payload?.assignments !== null && typeof payload?.assignments === 'object' && !Array.isArray(payload.assignments)) {
    78	        out.roles.assignments = payload.assignments;
    79	      } else {
    80	        out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: refusalNote('roles.remap', payload?.assignments) });
    81	      }
    82	      break;
    83	    case 'task.update': {
    84	      /** @type {Record<string, any>} */
    85	      const patch = {};
    86	      if (typeof payload?.goal === 'string') patch.goal = payload.goal;
    87	      if (Array.isArray(payload?.constraints) && payload.constraints.every((/** @type {any} */ c) => typeof c === 'string')) {
    88	        patch.constraints = payload.constraints;
    89	      }
    90	      if (Array.isArray(payload?.acceptance)) patch.acceptance = payload.acceptance;
    91	      if (Object.keys(patch).length === 0) {
    92	        out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: refusalNote('task.update', payload) });
    93	        break;
    94	      }
    95	      out.task = { ...out.task, ...patch };
    96	      break;
    97	    }
    98	    case 'git.update':
    99	      if (payload === null || typeof payload === 'object') out.git = payload;
   100	      else out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: refusalNote('git.update', payload) });
   101	      break;
   102	    case 'bundle.seed':
   103	      // Self-applying identity (gate-2 iter-2 B6): the auto-seed emits this as
   104	      // the journal's first event so a journal-only rebuild (snapshot + .bak
   105	      // both gone) restores the bundle's identity/origin/task instead of the
   106	      // "unknown" placeholder resolveBase seeds. Replayed first (lowest seq),
   107	      // so later events layer on top; on normal replay its seq is below the
   108	      // snapshot's journalSeq and it is skipped.
   109	      if (payload && typeof payload === 'object') {
   110	        if (typeof payload.bundleId === 'string') out.bundleId = payload.bundleId;
   111	        if (typeof payload.generation === 'number') out.generation = payload.generation;
   112	        if (typeof payload.createdAt === 'string') out.createdAt = payload.createdAt;
   113	        // Restore identity ONLY (platform/model) — never sessionHint/unstable.
   114	        // Session ownership is adopted AFTER the seed (a snapshot-only mutation),
   115	        // so replaying the seed must not clobber a later-adopted hint back to
   116	        // its seed-time null (gate-2 iter-2: caught by the adoption test).
   117	        if (payload.origin && typeof payload.origin === 'object') {
   118	          if (typeof payload.origin.platform === 'string') out.origin.platform = payload.origin.platform;
   119	          if (typeof payload.origin.model === 'string') out.origin.model = payload.origin.model;
   120	        }
   121	        if (payload.task && typeof payload.task === 'object' && typeof payload.task.goal === 'string') {
   122	          out.task.goal = payload.task.goal;
   123	        }
   124	      }
   125	      break;
   126	    case 'transcript.set':
   127	      // Opt-in transcript tail (plan §Transcript policy): stored under the
   128	      // literal property name `transcript` so purge-transcript strips it from
   129	      // every retained copy. Render never includes it.
   130	      if (payload?.transcript !== null && typeof payload?.transcript === 'object' && typeof payload.transcript.tail === 'string') {
   131	        out.transcript = {
   132	          capturedAt: typeof payload.transcript.capturedAt === 'string' ? payload.transcript.capturedAt : ts,
   133	          tail: payload.transcript.tail,
   134	        };
   135	      } else {
   136	        out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: refusalNote('transcript.set', 'malformed payload') });
   137	      }
   138	      break;
   139	    default:
   140	      out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: `[unhandled event type '${type}' recorded as note]` });
   141	      break;
   142	  }
   143	
   144	  out.updatedAt = ts;
   145	  // Harden against foreign/keyless entries (gate-2 fix): a missing seq must
   146	  // never poison journalSeq to NaN, and an absent dedupeKey must never enter
   147	  // the ring (undefined in the ring dedupes every later keyless event).
   148	  if (typeof seq === 'number' && Number.isFinite(seq)) out.journalSeq = Math.max(out.journalSeq, seq);
   149	  if (typeof event.dedupeKey === 'string' && event.dedupeKey.length > 0) {
   150	    out.dedupeRing.push(event.dedupeKey);
   151	    if (out.dedupeRing.length > RING_CAP) out.dedupeRing.splice(0, out.dedupeRing.length - RING_CAP);
   152	  }
   153	
   154	  return out;
   155	}
     1	import { newBundleId } from '../util/ids.mjs';
     2	import { jailRelPath } from '../util/jail.mjs';
     3	
     4	export const BUNDLE_SCHEMA = 'baton/bundle@1';
     5	const HANDOFF_STATUSES = ['open', 'sealed', 'received'];
     6	
     7	/**
     8	 * A fresh open bundle at generation 1.
     9	 * @param {{platform: string, model: string, goal: string}} input
    10	 * @param {string} nowIso
    11	 */
    12	export function emptyBundle({ platform, model, goal }, nowIso) {
    13	  return {
    14	    schema: BUNDLE_SCHEMA,
    15	    bundleId: newBundleId(),
    16	    generation: 1,
    17	    createdAt: nowIso,
    18	    updatedAt: nowIso,
    19	    origin: { platform, model, sessionHint: null, unstable: false },
    20	    task: { goal, constraints: [], acceptance: [] },
    21	    plan: { steps: [] },
    22	    decisions: [],
    23	    files: { touched: [] },
    24	    roles: { assignments: {} },
    25	    git: null,
    26	    handoff: {
    27	      status: 'open',
    28	      reason: null,
    29	      reasonClass: null,
    30	      toPlatformHint: null,
    31	      finalizedAt: null,
    32	      receive_log: [],
    33	    },
    34	    journalSeq: 0,
    35	    compaction: { droppedDecisions: 0, note: null },
    36	    dedupeRing: [],
    37	  };
    38	}
    39	
    40	/**
    41	 * Structural validation, deep (gate-2 fix 6): bundles are untrusted input, so
    42	 * every nested shape that render or the commands consume is checked — a string
    43	 * task.constraints must fail here, not crash renderHandoffMd — and file paths
    44	 * are jailed to the repository. Accumulates one {path, msg} per problem;
    45	 * unknown extra fields are tolerated everywhere (forward compatibility).
    46	 * @param {any} obj
    47	 * @returns {{ok: boolean, errors: {path: string, msg: string}[]}}
    48	 */
    49	export function validateBundle(obj) {
    50	  /** @type {{path: string, msg: string}[]} */
    51	  const errors = [];
    52	  const err = (/** @type {string} */ path, /** @type {string} */ msg) => errors.push({ path, msg });
    53	
    54	  if (obj === null || typeof obj !== 'object') {
    55	    return { ok: false, errors: [{ path: '', msg: 'bundle must be an object' }] };
    56	  }
    57	
    58	  if (obj.schema !== BUNDLE_SCHEMA) err('schema', `expected "${BUNDLE_SCHEMA}", got ${JSON.stringify(obj.schema)}`);
    59	  if (typeof obj.bundleId !== 'string') err('bundleId', 'must be a string');
    60	  if (typeof obj.generation !== 'number') err('generation', 'must be a number');
    61	  if (typeof obj.createdAt !== 'string') err('createdAt', 'must be an ISO string');
    62	  if (typeof obj.updatedAt !== 'string') err('updatedAt', 'must be an ISO string');
    63	  if (typeof obj.journalSeq !== 'number') err('journalSeq', 'must be a number');
    64	  if (!Array.isArray(obj.decisions)) {
    65	    err('decisions', 'must be an array');
    66	  } else {
    67	    obj.decisions.forEach((/** @type {any} */ d, /** @type {number} */ i) => {
    68	      if (d === null || typeof d !== 'object') err(`decisions[${i}]`, 'must be an object');
    69	    });
    70	  }
    71	  if (!Array.isArray(obj.dedupeRing)) err('dedupeRing', 'must be an array');
    72	
    73	  if (obj.origin === null || typeof obj.origin !== 'object') {
    74	    err('origin', 'must be an object');
    75	  } else if (typeof obj.origin.platform !== 'string') {
    76	    err('origin.platform', 'must be a string');
    77	  }
    78	
    79	  if (obj.task === null || typeof obj.task !== 'object') {
    80	    err('task', 'must be an object');
    81	  } else {
    82	    if (typeof obj.task.goal !== 'string') err('task.goal', 'must be a string');
    83	    if (!Array.isArray(obj.task.constraints)) {
    84	      err('task.constraints', 'must be an array of strings');
    85	    } else {
    86	      obj.task.constraints.forEach((/** @type {any} */ c, /** @type {number} */ i) => {
    87	        if (typeof c !== 'string') err(`task.constraints[${i}]`, 'must be a string');
    88	      });
    89	    }
    90	    if (!Array.isArray(obj.task.acceptance)) err('task.acceptance', 'must be an array');
    91	  }
    92	
    93	  if (obj.plan === null || typeof obj.plan !== 'object') {
    94	    err('plan', 'must be an object');
    95	  } else if (!Array.isArray(obj.plan.steps)) {
    96	    err('plan.steps', 'must be an array');
    97	  } else {
    98	    obj.plan.steps.forEach((/** @type {any} */ s, /** @type {number} */ i) => {
    99	      if (s === null || typeof s !== 'object') err(`plan.steps[${i}]`, 'must be an object');
   100	    });
   101	  }
   102	
   103	  if (obj.files === null || typeof obj.files !== 'object') {
   104	    err('files', 'must be an object');
   105	  } else if (!Array.isArray(obj.files.touched)) {
   106	    err('files.touched', 'must be an array');
   107	  } else {
   108	    obj.files.touched.forEach((/** @type {any} */ f, /** @type {number} */ i) => {
   109	      if (f === null || typeof f !== 'object') err(`files.touched[${i}]`, 'must be an object');
   110	      else if (jailRelPath(f.path) === null) {
   111	        err(`files.touched[${i}].path`, 'must be a normalized repo-relative path (absolute and escaping paths are refused)');
   112	      }
   113	    });
   114	  }
   115	
   116	  if (obj.roles === null || typeof obj.roles !== 'object') {
   117	    err('roles', 'must be an object');
   118	  } else if (obj.roles.assignments === null || typeof obj.roles.assignments !== 'object' || Array.isArray(obj.roles.assignments)) {
   119	    err('roles.assignments', 'must be an object');
   120	  } else {
   121	    // Each assignment value is consumed by renderHandoffMd (reads .platform,
   122	    // .model, .mode) — a null/non-object value passed validation and crashed
   123	    // render (gate-2 iter-2 B4). Every value must be a plain object.
   124	    for (const [role, a] of Object.entries(obj.roles.assignments)) {
   125	      if (a === null || typeof a !== 'object' || Array.isArray(a)) err(`roles.assignments.${role}`, 'must be an object');
   126	    }
   127	  }
   128	  if (obj.compaction === null || typeof obj.compaction !== 'object') err('compaction', 'must be an object');
   129	
   130	  if (obj.handoff === null || typeof obj.handoff !== 'object') {
   131	    err('handoff', 'must be an object');
   132	  } else {
   133	    if (!HANDOFF_STATUSES.includes(obj.handoff.status)) {
   134	      err('handoff.status', `must be one of ${HANDOFF_STATUSES.join('|')}`);
   135	    }
   136	    if (obj.handoff.receive_log !== undefined && !Array.isArray(obj.handoff.receive_log)) {
   137	      err('handoff.receive_log', 'must be an array');
   138	    }
   139	  }
   140	
   141	  // git is nullable by design; when present it must be an object.
   142	  if (obj.git !== null && obj.git !== undefined && typeof obj.git !== 'object') err('git', 'must be an object or null');
   143	
   144	  return { ok: errors.length === 0, errors };
   145	}
   146	
   147	/**
   148	 * Version migration seam. Schema v1 is identity; anything else is unsupported.
   149	 * @param {any} obj
   150	 */
   151	export function migrate(obj) {
   152	  if (obj?.schema === BUNDLE_SCHEMA) return structuredClone(obj);
   153	  throw new Error(`unsupported bundle schema ${JSON.stringify(obj?.schema)}; this baton understands ${BUNDLE_SCHEMA}`);
   154	}

     1	import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
     2	
     3	// Git facts degrade to null rather than block a checkpoint: not-a-repo,
     4	// missing binary, and timeouts are all expected environments (plan §Root
     5	// discovery & degraded modes).
     6	const GIT_TIMEOUT_MS = 5000;
     7	// The untracked digest is bounded: beyond this many paths, entries fold in by
     8	// name only, keeping prepare-time hashing cheap on huge untracked trees.
     9	const UNTRACKED_CAP = 200;
    10	// NUL separator: cannot occur in diffs, sizes, hex hashes, or file paths, so
    11	// joined digest inputs cannot collide across field boundaries.
    12	const SEP = String.fromCharCode(0);
    13	const NEWLINE = String.fromCharCode(10);
    14	
    15	const sha256 = (/** @type {string} */ s) => createHash('sha256').update(s).digest('hex');
    16	
    17	/**
    18	 * Capture the repo's git state plus a content-sensitive digest. The digest
    19	 * covers the staged diff, unstaged diff, and a bounded untracked-files digest
    20	 * (path + size + content hash) — NOT the HEAD sha, which the receive token
    21	 * binds as its own input, so a HEAD-only move is distinguishable from a
    22	 * content change.
    23	 * @param {{execFile: Function, cwd: string, fs: any}} deps
    24	 * @returns {Promise<{branch: string, headSha: string, dirty: boolean, dirtySummary: string[], contentDigest: string} | null>}
    25	 */
    26	export async function snapshot({ execFile, cwd, fs }) {
    27	  const git = (/** @type {string[]} */ args) => execFile('git', args, { cwd, timeout: GIT_TIMEOUT_MS });
    28	  let branch, head, status, staged, unstaged, untrackedList;
    29	  try {
    30	    [branch, head, status, staged, unstaged, untrackedList] = await Promise.all([
    31	      git(['rev-parse', '--abbrev-ref', 'HEAD']),
    32	      git(['rev-parse', 'HEAD']),
    33	      git(['status', '--porcelain']),
    34	      git(['diff', '--cached']),
    35	      git(['diff']),
    36	      git(['ls-files', '--others', '--exclude-standard']),
    37	    ]);
    38	  } catch {
    39	    return null;
    40	  }
    41	
    42	  const nonEmptyLines = (/** @type {string} */ s) => s.split(NEWLINE).filter((/** @type {string} */ l) => l !== '');
    43	  const dirtySummary = nonEmptyLines(status.stdout);
    44	  const untrackedPaths = nonEmptyLines(untrackedList.stdout);
    45	
    46	  const untrackedEntries = untrackedPaths.map((rel, i) => {
    47	    if (i >= UNTRACKED_CAP) return [rel, 'capped'].join(SEP);
    48	    try {
    49	      const content = fs.readFileSync(cwd + '/' + rel, 'utf8');
    50	      return [rel, String(Buffer.byteLength(content)), sha256(content)].join(SEP);
    51	    } catch {
    52	      // Listed but unreadable (deleted mid-flight): still digest its presence.
    53	      return [rel, 'absent'].join(SEP);
    54	    }
    55	  });
    56	
    57	  return {
    58	    branch: branch.stdout.trim(),
    59	    headSha: head.stdout.trim(),
    60	    dirty: dirtySummary.length > 0,
    61	    dirtySummary,
    62	    contentDigest: sha256([staged.stdout, unstaged.stdout, ...untrackedEntries].join(SEP)),
    63	  };
    64	}

     1	import { probeRegexSafe } from './probe.mjs';
     2	
     3	const PLATFORMS = ['claude-code', 'codex', 'cursor'];
     4	const CLASSES = ['usage-limit', 'auth', 'throttle', 'other-error'];
     5	const MATCHER_KINDS = ['substring', 'regex', 'json-field'];
     6	const REGEX_MAX_LEN = 200;
     7	
     8	/**
     9	 * Is a quantified group's body ambiguous? Alternation, another quantifier, or
    10	 * any nested group inside a `(…)+`/`(…)*`/`(…){` makes backtracking
    11	 * super-linear in the worst case — conservatively rejected (gate-2 fix 6:
    12	 * the old lint accepted `(a|aa)+$` and the `(a|a)*x` live repro).
    13	 * @param {string} body
    14	 */
    15	function riskyGroupBody(body) {
    16	  // Strip a non-capture/lookaround/named marker so `(?:abc)+` reads as `abc`.
    17	  const b = body.replace(/^\?(?:<=|<!|:|=|!|<[^>]*>)/, '');
    18	  let inClass = false;
    19	  for (let i = 0; i < b.length; i++) {
    20	    const c = b[i];
    21	    if (c === '\\') {
    22	      i++;
    23	      continue;
    24	    }
    25	    if (inClass) {
    26	      if (c === ']') inClass = false;
    27	      continue;
    28	    }
    29	    if (c === '[') {
    30	      inClass = true;
    31	      continue;
    32	    }
    33	    if (c === '|' || c === '+' || c === '*' || c === '{' || c === '?' || c === '(' || c === ')') return true;
    34	  }
    35	  return false;
    36	}
    37	
    38	/**
    39	 * Structural scan: walk the pattern (class- and escape-aware), and for every
    40	 * group followed by an unbounded quantifier check its full body — any nesting
    41	 * depth — for ambiguity. Returns a problem string or null.
    42	 * @param {string} pattern
    43	 */
    44	function scanQuantifiedGroups(pattern) {
    45	  /** @type {number[]} */
    46	  const opens = [];
    47	  let inClass = false;
    48	  for (let i = 0; i < pattern.length; i++) {
    49	    const c = pattern[i];
    50	    if (c === '\\') {
    51	      i++;
    52	      continue;
    53	    }
    54	    if (inClass) {
    55	      if (c === ']') inClass = false;
    56	      continue;
    57	    }
    58	    if (c === '[') {
    59	      inClass = true;
    60	      continue;
    61	    }
    62	    if (c === '(') {
    63	      opens.push(i);
    64	      continue;
    65	    }
    66	    if (c === ')') {
    67	      const start = opens.pop();
    68	      if (start === undefined) continue; // unbalanced — new RegExp() reports it
    69	      const next = pattern[i + 1];
    70	      if (next === '+' || next === '*' || next === '{') {
    71	        const body = pattern.slice(start + 1, i);
    72	        if (riskyGroupBody(body)) {
    73	          return `quantified group "(${body})" contains alternation, another quantifier, or a nested group`;
    74	        }
    75	      }
    76	    }
    77	  }
    78	  return null;
    79	}
    80	
    81	const ALLOWED_FLAGS = 'dgimsuvy';
    82	
    83	/**
    84	 * Validate a regex flags string (gate-2 iter-2 B3): every char must be an
    85	 * allowed JS flag with no duplicates, so a flags value that compiles nowhere
    86	 * (`z`, `ii`) is rejected at LOAD, not left to throw later inside classify().
    87	 * @param {string} id @param {string} flags
    88	 */
    89	function lintFlags(id, flags) {
    90	  if (typeof flags !== 'string') throw new Error(`signature ${id}: regex flags must be a string`);
    91	  const seen = new Set();
    92	  for (const c of flags) {
    93	    if (!ALLOWED_FLAGS.includes(c)) throw new Error(`signature ${id}: unsupported regex flag "${c}" (allowed: ${ALLOWED_FLAGS})`);
    94	    if (seen.has(c)) throw new Error(`signature ${id}: duplicate regex flag "${c}"`);
    95	    seen.add(c);
    96	  }
    97	}
    98	
    99	/**
   100	 * Lint one regex pattern together with its flags. With the load-time worker
   101	 * probe on overlay tables (see probe.mjs), the runtime time-guard is by
   102	 * construction: patterns that could backtrack catastrophically never reach
   103	 * classify(), which stays synchronous with no timers.
   104	 * @param {string} id @param {string} pattern @param {string} [flags]
   105	 */
   106	function lintRegex(id, pattern, flags = '') {
   107	  if (typeof pattern !== 'string' || pattern.length > REGEX_MAX_LEN) {
   108	    throw new Error(`signature ${id}: regex pattern exceeds the ${REGEX_MAX_LEN}-char length cap (or is not a string)`);
   109	  }
   110	  if (/\\[1-9]/.test(pattern)) {
   111	    throw new Error(`signature ${id}: regex backreferences are not allowed`);
   112	  }
   113	  lintFlags(id, flags);
   114	  const problem = scanQuantifiedGroups(pattern);
   115	  if (problem) {
   116	    throw new Error(`signature ${id}: ${problem} (pathological backtracking / non-linear-time risk)`);
   117	  }
   118	  try {
   119	    // eslint-disable-next-line no-new
   120	    new RegExp(pattern, flags);
   121	  } catch (err) {
   122	    throw new Error(`signature ${id}: invalid regex pattern/flags (${/** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? 'syntax error'})`);
   123	  }
   124	}
   125	
   126	/** @param {any} s */
   127	function lintSignature(s) {
   128	  if (typeof s.id !== 'string' || s.id.length === 0) throw new Error('signature with missing id');
   129	  if (!PLATFORMS.includes(s.platform)) {
   130	    throw new Error(`signature ${s.id}: unknown platform "${s.platform}" (expected ${PLATFORMS.join('|')})`);
   131	  }
   132	  if (!CLASSES.includes(s.class)) throw new Error(`signature ${s.id}: unknown class "${s.class}"`);
   133	  if (!s.matcher || !MATCHER_KINDS.includes(s.matcher.kind)) {
   134	    throw new Error(`signature ${s.id}: unknown matcher kind "${s.matcher?.kind}" (expected ${MATCHER_KINDS.join('|')})`);
   135	  }
   136	  if (s.matcher.kind === 'regex') lintRegex(s.id, s.matcher.pattern, s.matcher.flags ?? '');
   137	  if (s.matcher.kind === 'substring' && typeof s.matcher.value !== 'string') {
   138	    throw new Error(`signature ${s.id}: substring matcher needs a string value`);
   139	  }
   140	  if (s.matcher.kind === 'json-field' && typeof s.matcher.path !== 'string') {
   141	    throw new Error(`signature ${s.id}: json-field matcher needs a dotted string path`);
   142	  }
   143	  if (s.resetHint !== undefined) lintRegex(s.id, s.resetHint);
   144	}
   145	
   146	/** @param {any} fs @param {string} path @param {{probe?: boolean}} [opts] */
   147	function readTable(fs, path, opts = {}) {
   148	  const raw = fs.readFileSync(path, 'utf8');
   149	  const table = JSON.parse(raw);
   150	  if (table?.schema !== 'baton/signatures@1') {
   151	    throw new Error(`signature table at ${path}: expected schema baton/signatures@1, got ${JSON.stringify(table?.schema)}`);
   152	  }
   153	  if (!Array.isArray(table.signatures)) throw new Error(`signature table at ${path}: signatures must be an array`);
   154	  for (const s of table.signatures) {
   155	    lintSignature(s);
   156	    // Overlay tables are untrusted: the structural lint cannot see group-free
   157	    // pathological shapes (chained stars), so every overlay regex additionally
   158	    // runs against adversarial inputs in a worker with a hard deadline.
   159	    if (opts.probe === true) {
   160	      for (const pattern of [s.matcher?.kind === 'regex' ? s.matcher.pattern : null, s.resetHint ?? null]) {
   161	        if (typeof pattern !== 'string') continue;
   162	        const verdict = probeRegexSafe(pattern, s.matcher?.flags ?? '');
   163	        if (!verdict.safe) throw new Error(`signature ${s.id}: ${verdict.reason}`);
   164	      }
   165	    }
   166	  }
   167	  return table;
   168	}
   169	
   170	/**
   171	 * Load + validate the signature table, merging an optional overlay by id
   172	 * (later wins — the drift-absorbing hot-patch surface). Builtin patterns are
   173	 * package-shipped and statically linted; overlay patterns are additionally
   174	 * probe-guarded at load.
   175	 * @param {{builtinPath: string, overlayPath?: string}} paths @param {any} io
   176	 */
   177	export function loadSignatures({ builtinPath, overlayPath }, io) {
   178	  const base = readTable(io.fs, builtinPath);
   179	  if (!overlayPath) return base;
   180	
   181	  const overlay = readTable(io.fs, overlayPath, { probe: true });
   182	  const merged = [...base.signatures];
   183	  for (const s of overlay.signatures) {
   184	    const i = merged.findIndex((b) => b.id === s.id);
   185	    if (i === -1) merged.push(s);
   186	    else merged[i] = s;
   187	  }
   188	  return { ...base, updated: overlay.updated ?? base.updated, signatures: merged };
   189	}
     1	import { Worker } from 'node:worker_threads';
     2	
     3	// Load-time pathological-regex probe (plan §Threat model; gate-2 fix 6). The
     4	// structural lint in signatures.mjs rejects every quantified-group shape it
     5	// can see, but a pattern with no groups at all (e.g. a chained-star `a*a*…x`)
     6	// can still backtrack combinatorially. This probe runs the candidate against
     7	// adversarial inputs inside a worker thread and enforces a hard wall-clock
     8	// deadline via Atomics.wait — a synchronous regex match cannot be interrupted
     9	// in-process, so isolation is the only reliable bound. Overlay tables pass
    10	// through this at load; the classify() hot path stays synchronous because
    11	// every table it sees was probed here first.
    12	
    13	const DEADLINE_MS = 1000; // generous vs worker spawn (~50-100 ms); tiny vs the 93.8 s repro
    14	const INPUT_LEN = 4096;
    15	
    16	// A covering alphabet (gate-2 iter-2 M8): a chained-star pattern backtracks on
    17	// a long run of characters its classes accept, then a non-matching tail. The
    18	// old probe drew fill chars only from the pattern's LITERAL [A-Za-z0-9], so a
    19	// group-free star over \d / \s / [!-/] never got a matching input and was
    20	// reported safe, then hung classify(). These fills exercise digits, spaces,
    21	// word chars, and common punctuation ranges regardless of how the class is
    22	// spelled; the tail (\x01) is outside all of them, forcing a full backtrack.
    23	const COVER = ['0', '9', ' ', '\t', 'a', 'Z', '_', '!', '/', '-', '.', '#', '@'];
    24	const TAIL = String.fromCharCode(1);
    25	
    26	/**
    27	 * Build adversarial probe inputs: one long run per covering-alphabet char plus
    28	 * the pattern's own literals, each ending in a non-matching byte.
    29	 * @param {string} pattern
    30	 * @returns {string[]}
    31	 */
    32	function probeInputs(pattern) {
    33	  const literals = [...new Set(pattern.match(/[A-Za-z0-9]/g) ?? [])].slice(0, 4);
    34	  const fills = [...new Set([...COVER, ...literals])];
    35	  return fills.map((c) => c.repeat(INPUT_LEN) + TAIL);
    36	}
    37	
    38	/**
    39	 * @param {string} pattern @param {string} [flags] @param {number} [timeoutMs]
    40	 * @returns {{safe: true} | {safe: false, reason: string}}
    41	 */
    42	export function probeRegexSafe(pattern, flags = '', timeoutMs = DEADLINE_MS) {
    43	  // Pre-compile in the main thread (gate-2 iter-2 B3): a bad pattern/flag pair
    44	  // is a COMPILE error reported distinctly, never conflated with a timeout.
    45	  try {
    46	    // eslint-disable-next-line no-new
    47	    new RegExp(pattern, flags);
    48	  } catch (err) {
    49	    return { safe: false, reason: `invalid regex pattern/flags (compile error: ${/** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? 'syntax error'})` };
    50	  }
    51	  const sab = new SharedArrayBuffer(4);
    52	  const flag = new Int32Array(sab);
    53	  const worker = new Worker(new URL('./probe-worker.mjs', import.meta.url), {
    54	    workerData: { pattern, flags, inputs: probeInputs(pattern), sab },
    55	  });
    56	  const waited = Atomics.wait(flag, 0, 0, timeoutMs);
    57	  worker.terminate();
    58	  worker.unref();
    59	  if (waited === 'timed-out') {
    60	    return { safe: false, reason: `pathological backtracking: probe match exceeded the ${timeoutMs} ms deadline` };
    61	  }
    62	  return { safe: true };
    63	}
     1	// Worker side of the regex pathological probe (see probe.mjs). Runs the
     2	// candidate pattern against adversarial inputs in an isolated thread; the main
     3	// thread waits on the shared flag with a hard deadline and terminates this
     4	// worker if the matches never finish. flag[0]: 0 = running, 1 = done.
     5	import { workerData } from 'node:worker_threads';
     6	
     7	const { pattern, flags, inputs, sab } = workerData;
     8	const flag = new Int32Array(sab);
     9	
    10	try {
    11	  const re = new RegExp(pattern, flags);
    12	  for (const s of inputs) re.test(s);
    13	} catch {
    14	  // A compile error is not a hang; the lint layer reports syntax separately.
    15	}
    16	
    17	Atomics.store(flag, 0, 1);
    18	Atomics.notify(flag, 0);

    36	/** Numeric segment-wise version compare: negative when a < b. */
    37	function cmpVersions(/** @type {string} */ a, /** @type {string} */ b) {
    38	  const as = a.split('.').map(Number);
    39	  const bs = b.split('.').map(Number);
    40	  for (let i = 0; i < Math.max(as.length, bs.length); i++) {
    41	    const d = (as[i] ?? 0) - (bs[i] ?? 0);
    42	    if (d !== 0) return d < 0 ? -1 : 1;
    43	  }
    44	  return 0;
    45	}
