# Test-verifier verdict — gate-2 iteration-2 fold (consolidated)

- **Role**: test-verifier
- **Model**: gpt-5.5 @ xhigh (`codex exec -s read-only`)
- **Harness**: codex CLI, read-only sandbox
- **Date**: 2026-07-12
- **Scope**: `git diff a0e3dcd..HEAD -- tests/` (12 files, ~1090 lines) — the iteration-2 fix wave for findings B1-B6, M1-M8, B5, m1
- **Degraded**: no (single reviewer by design — test-verifier role is single-model)
- **Verdict**: **BLOCKED** — 2 blocking, 3 major

## Findings

1. **[blocking]** `tests/unit/lock-hardening.test.mjs:39` — the B1 "two contenders" test is sequential; the strong check is white-box rm/mkdir instrumentation, so it would not fail on a real double-publish stale-lock race. Fix: barrier-released real-process test, one dead owner + two reclaimers, assert persisted owner/journal proves exactly one takeover.
2. **[blocking]** `tests/commands/majors-minors.test.mjs:185` — B2's failing-hook log path is tested only on a normal tree; a logger writing through a symlinked `.handoff/log` would still pass. Fix: real-fs symlink test for hook-failure logging that asserts fail-open and no write through the linked path.
3. **[major]** `tests/commands/gate2-iter2-group6.test.mjs:129` — M4 low-confidence avoidance assertion is vacuous: the config never requires selecting cursor before an earlier eligible entry. Fix: role chain with cursor first-eligible; assert low-confidence keeps it selectable while high-confidence intake avoids it.
4. **[major]** `tests/commands/gate2-iter2-group6.test.mjs:57` — M2 tests `jailRelPath` but not the Windows `checkHandoffTree` realpath containment. Fix: fake Windows `realpathSync` cases (in-tree, outside-root, cross-volume).
5. **[major]** `tests/integration/receiver-concurrency.test.mjs:83` — M7 races two commits on the *same* prepared receipt, not two independently-prepared receipts from one sealed state. Fix: prepare two distinct receipts, release both from one barrier, assert one wins and loser state is untouched.

The read-only Gate 2 re-review is complete. Targeted validation is green, but multiple code-level and reproduced defects keep the gate blocked.

codex
Gate 2 remains blocked. The runnable validation passed: 81 targeted tests and typecheck are green; attribution is clean. Six prior dispositions remain incomplete, and the changed surface introduces additional defects.

VERDICT: BLOCKED
DISPOSITIONS: 7 of 13 iteration-2 findings verified closed; NOT closed: 1, 2, 6, 7, 8, 11
FINDINGS:
1. [blocking] — core/src/bundle/lock.mjs:64-93,170-186 — the rename winner calls `journalNote` after freeing the canonical lock path but before reacquiring it; another process can own the lock and allocate the same journal sequence concurrently, violating mutual exclusion even though exactly one contender wins the stale-directory rename — plan §Concurrency atomic lock acquisition; iteration-2 finding #3 — Fix: record the arbitration in an atomic pending-reclaim marker, then append it to the main journal only after acquiring the canonical lock.
2. [blocking] — core/src/bundle/merge.mjs:76-79 — replay accepts `roles.remap` assignments containing null values; `loadBundle` therefore returns an invalid bundle and `renderHandoffMd` crashes at core/src/bundle/render.mjs:59 — plan §Threat model untrusted inputs; iteration-2 finding #6 — Fix: deeply validate each event payload before applying it, or validate the candidate post-event bundle and skip malformed events with a warning.
3. [blocking] — core/src/commands/doctor.mjs:229-263 — doctor writes `.handoff/log/probe-cache.json` without the managed-tree jail or repo lock, so a symlinked `.handoff` or `log` directory can redirect the write outside the repository — plan §Threat model symlink refusal; iteration-2 finding #4 — Fix: perform cache creation and replacement inside `withLock` after `assertHandoffTreeSafe`.
4. [major] — core/src/commands/checkpoint.mjs:286-293 — when git capture fails, a rewritten checkpoint preserves the previous git object without any unavailable marker or warning; a direct repro retained `oldsha` as if current — plan §Root discovery & degraded modes; iteration-2 finding #1 — Fix: replace stale git data with an explicit unavailable state and surface a warning whenever the refresh fails.
5. [major] — core/src/commands/shared.mjs:48-53; core/src/util/jail.mjs:60-62 — Windows drive-root repositories remain broken: ascent never examines `C:/`, and a `C:/` root produces an expected `C://.handoff`, rejecting the real `C:/.handoff`; UNC ascent also lacks a share-root ceiling — plan §Root discovery and Windows + packaging; iteration-2 finding #2 — Fix: use volume-aware `path.win32.parse/dirname/relative` operations and normalize trailing root separators.
6. [major] — core/src/commands/doctor.mjs:73-105; core/src/roles/resolve.mjs:20-33 — doctor still does not report installed/enabled/trusted/observed as four distinct states, and a successful Claude `--version` record (`installed`, `ok`) makes Claude ineligible as “unauthenticated” instead of selectable-but-degraded; authenticated/error selections also lack degradation — plan §Role matrix availability probing and §Codex/Cursor trust gates; iteration-2 finding #7 — Fix: represent verified-false separately from unknown for each capability and hook dimension, and degrade rather than skip unknown dimensions.
7. [major] — core/src/receive/txn.mjs:125-145,201-227 — prepare correctly declines to avoid on a low-confidence match, but commit promotes that same heuristic to `reasonClass:"usage-limit"` and carries it into the next open generation; the next receive then trusts it as high confidence and silently avoids the new origin — plan §Limit detector low-confidence behavior; iteration-2 finding #8 — Fix: persist confidence with the classification, never seal a low-confidence class without confirmation, and clear prior-generation handoff reason metadata when opening the new generation.
8. [major] — core/src/cli.mjs:108-110 — subcommand help bypasses JSON emission: `baton status --help --json` exits successfully with raw usage text rather than one envelope — plan §CLI surface; iteration-2 finding #11 — Fix: route the subcommand-help branch through `emitEnvelope` whenever global JSON intent is set.
9. [major] — core/src/detect/probe.mjs:23-35 — the fixed `U+0001` probe tail is not guaranteed to be non-matching; an overlay ending in that byte with twelve group-free `.*` atoms passes the probe in about 20 ms, while matching 512 ordinary characters did not finish within one second — plan §Limit detector hardening; findings-iter2 M8 — Fix: structurally reject overlapping chained quantified atoms, with this terminal-byte case as a regression test, rather than treating a finite probe alphabet as a runtime bound.
10. [major] — core/src/commands/checkpoint.mjs:48-63 — transcript containment compares Windows backslash realpaths against a forward-slash suffix, so every nested allowed transcript is refused on Windows; a Windows-spelled in-repo repro captured nothing — plan §Threat model and Windows + packaging; findings-iter2 m1 — Fix: separator-normalize both realpaths and use volume-aware relative containment.
11. [minor] — core/src/commands/receive.mjs:15-39 — `--reason-class` is accepted as trusted explicit intake without validating the class enum, unlike finalize, and can be bound into a receipt and persisted verbatim — plan §Limit detector class vocabulary; iteration-2 finding #8 — Fix: reject values outside `usage-limit|auth|throttle|other-error` before prepare or commit.
